Our main technical realization is a latent variational integrator: LaWM encodes observations into learned generalized coordinates, learns a latent discrete Lagrangian over consecutive latent states, constructs a discrete action functional, and advances prediction by solving the corresponding discrete integration condition. Thus, physical structure is not merely used to score, regularize, or constrain a completed trajectory; it defines the latent transition rule itself. Because the transition is induced by a discrete variational principle, LaWM provides a structure-preserving bias for long-horizon visual prediction. Across physics-clean synthetic dynamics and embodied robot interaction benchmarks, LaWM improves physical invariance, background consistency, motion smoothness, and appearance and geometric prediction metrics over video-generation and world-model baselines.

</details>


### [38] [Beyond the False Trade-off: Adaptive EWC for Stealthy and Generalizable T2I Backdoors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08280)
*Lu Bowen,Xinyu Tang,Yin Yin Low,Shu-Min Leong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出余弦感知自适应EWC方法，用于文本到图像后门攻击中平衡攻击成功率与模型保真度，解决了静态EWC在弱触发器上性能下降的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于输出蒸馏（如LwF）的方法正则化效果有限，难以在保持模型保真度的同时实现隐蔽的后门攻击；静态EWC在弱触发器上存在攻击成功率与保真度之间的人工权衡。

Method: 提出Cosine-Aware Adaptive EWC，采用余弦语义效用函数动态调整EWC正则化强度，结合自适应调度机制，将固定惩罚转变为上下文敏感的约束。

Result: 在攻击成功率与模型保真度之间取得更优平衡，在域外数据集上表现出更强的鲁棒性，优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 余弦感知自适应EWC能有效解决静态EWC中ASR与保真度的权衡问题，为文本到图像后门攻击提供更优的保真度保持方案。

Abstract: Preserving model fidelity is essential for stealthy text-to-image (T2I) backdoor attacks. Existing methods such as Learning without Forgetting (LwF) rely on output-based distillation, which provides limited regularization. We introduce Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC) as a parameter-based alternative for preserving fidelity in backdoor learning. While stronger in principle, we show that standard static EWC with a fixed regularization weight lambda and mean-squared utility loss creates an artificial trade-off between attack success rate (ASR) and fidelity, particularly degrading performance on weak triggers. To address this, we propose Cosine-Aware Adaptive EWC, which dynamically adjusts EWC regularization using a cosine-based semantic utility and adaptive scheduling. This approach transforms EWC from a fixed penalty into a context-sensitive constraint, maintaining high ASR while preserving model fidelity. Experiments demonstrate improved ASR-fidelity balance and enhanced robustness on out-of-domain (OOD) datasets compared to existing baselines.

</details>


### [39] [HTPO: Towards Exploration-Exploitation Balanced Policy Optimization via Hierarchical Token-level Objective Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08283)
*Xincheng Yao,Ruoqi Li,Cheng Chen,Daoxin Zhang,Yi Wu,Yao Hu,Chongyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HTPO算法，通过层级化令牌级目标控制，对推理响应中的令牌进行功能分组（根据提示难度、答案正确性和令牌熵），并为不同组设计专门的优化目标，以实现探索与利用的更平衡权衡。在AIME'24和AIME'25上分别比强基线DAPO提升8.6%和6.7%，且随采样预算增加优势扩大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流RL算法在训练LLM进行CoT推理时，将所有令牌等同对待，赋予相同的优化目标，无法区分不同令牌在推理过程中的不同角色，导致缺乏有效机制动态平衡探索-利用权衡。

Method: 提出HTPO，采用分治思想，从提示难度、答案正确性和令牌熵三个维度将响应令牌层级化划分为特定功能组，在每个组内根据其对探索或利用的贡献设计专门的优化目标，从而实现令牌级别的自适应目标控制。

Result: 在多个挑战性推理基准上，HTPO显著超越强基线DAPO，在AIME'24和AIME'25上分别提升8.6%和6.7%；在增加测试时计算量时，性能优势随采样预算增加而扩大。

Conclusion: HTPO通过令牌级层次化控制方法，有效促进了探索而不牺牲利用性能，实现了更平衡的探索-利用权衡，验证了自适应令牌级目标控制的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a pivotal technique for enhancing the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, the de facto practice of mainstream RL algorithms is to treat all tokens of one response equally and assign the same optimization objective to each token, failing to provide granular guidance for the reasoning process. While in Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning, different tokens usually play distinct roles. Therefore, the current RL algorithms lack an effective mechanism to dynamically balance the exploration-exploitation trade-off during learning. To this end, we propose Hierarchical Token-level Objective Control Policy Optimization (HTPO), a novel RL algorithm that takes the divide-and-conquer idea to hierarchically partition the response tokens into specific functional groups from three aspects (i.e., prompt difficulty, answer correctness, and token entropy). Within each group, according to the contributions to exploration or exploitation, we design specialized optimization objectives to facilitate the effective execution of each token's expected functionality. In this way, HTPO can achieve a more balanced exploration-exploitation trade-off. Extensive experiments on challenging reasoning benchmarks validate the superiority of our HTPO algorithm, which significantly outperforms the strong DAPO baseline (e.g., +8.6% and +6.7% on AIME'24 and AIME'25, respectively). When scaling test-time compute, the HTPO-trained model maintains a consistent performance advantage over the DAPO baseline, and the gap widens as the sampling budget increases, validating that our adaptive token-level control method fosters effective exploration without sacrificing exploitation performance. Code will be at https://github.com/xcyao00/HTPO.

</details>


### [40] [Exactness Matters for Physical Rule Enforcement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08285)
*Bum Jun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了自回归科学预测模型中物理约束修复（operator exactness）的可靠性边界，发现精确投影（如傅里叶投影）能显著提升周期流域的预测精度，但近似约束修复（如基于泊松方程的边界保持）可能因分布偏移反而恶化误差；最佳修复策略取决于算子与目标几何的对齐程度，应在强约束前进行对齐基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归科学预测模型常通过修复每个预测状态来施加物理或结构约束，但强物理规则的施加何时可靠、何时引发分布偏移尚不清楚。本文旨在通过算子精确性（operator exactness）的概念系统研究这一问题。

Method: 定义算子精确性（repair map在目标流形上为单位映射且与目标几何对齐），比较原始预测、事后修复（post hoc）和循环内修复（in-loop）三种策略，在周期不可压缩Navier-Stokes流、非周期CFDBench流（腔流、管流、坝流、圆柱绕流）以及分层预测支持任务上进行实验，并引入目标畸变MSE、受控失配、筛选修复、自适应门控和外部骨架检查等分析手段。

Result: 在精确周期场景（NS-128基准）中，傅里叶投影大幅提升滚动精度：Raw-FNO的最终步MSE为(9.390±6.290)×10⁻⁵，事后投影降至(1.130±0.165)×10⁻⁶，循环内投影降至(5.370±0.113)×10⁻⁷。但在非周期场景中，更强泊松修复可降低散度但恶化滚动误差；目标畸变MSE比线性系统残差更能预测这种损害。近似场景的最优操作点可能是原始预测或近恒等映射。分层预测呈现相同模式。精确预测调和是稳定基线，而混合自上而下修复（经验证集调优的插值方法）依赖于数据集。

Conclusion: 约束施加应根据算子-数据对齐程度进行基准测试后再决定施加强度；精确投影在几何对齐时可靠，近似修复策略需谨慎评估其分布偏移风险。

Abstract: Autoregressive scientific forecasters often enforce physical or structural constraints by repairing each predicted state before feeding it back into the model. However, it remains unclear when stronger physical rule enforcement becomes reliable and when it becomes a source of distribution shift. We study this question through operator exactness, meaning whether the repair map is the identity on the target manifold and is aligned with the target geometry. We compare raw forecasting, post hoc repair, and in-loop repair across periodic incompressible Navier--Stokes, non-periodic CFDBench flows, and a hierarchical-forecasting support task. In the exact periodic regime, Fourier projection substantially improves rollout accuracy. On the NS-128 benchmark, a strong Raw-FNO has a final-step rollout MSE at horizon 100 of $(9.390 \pm 6.290)\times 10^{-5}$, and post hoc and in-loop projection reduce it to $(1.130 \pm 0.165)\times 10^{-6}$ and $(5.370 \pm 0.113)\times 10^{-7}$. However, once an exact projection is unavailable and only approximate boundary-preserving cleanup is available, the ordering changes. Across cavity, tube, dam, and cylinder flow, stronger Poisson-based cleanup can reduce divergence while worsening rollout error; target-distortion MSE predicts this harm far better than a linear-system residual. Controlled mismatch, screened cleanup, adaptive gating, and external-backbone checks show that the best approximate-regime operating point can be raw or near-identity. Hierarchical forecasting gives the same broader pattern. Exact forecast reconciliation is a stable baseline, whereas blended top-down repair, a validation-tuned interpolation toward historical-proportion top-down reconciliation, is dataset-dependent. Thus, constraint enforcement should be benchmarked by operator--data alignment before enforcement strength.

</details>


### [41] [Multi-Armed Bandits With Best-Action Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08287)
*Francesco Bacchiocchi,Matteo Castiglioni,Alberto Marchesi,Francesco Emanuele Stradi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在bandit-feedback模型下，带有最佳动作查询的Multi-Armed Bandits问题。结果表明，当奖励在臂之间相关时，全反馈模型中的改进无法推广，存在Ω(√(T-k))的下界；而在i.i.d.随机奖励下，可实现Õ(min{T/k, √(T-k)})的遗憾，并匹配了对数因子内的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Russo等人[2024]在全反馈模型中证明了k次最佳动作查询能将遗憾从Õ(√T)降低到Õ(min{T/k, √T})。但这一改进能否推广到更现实的bandit-feedback模型（学习者只能观察所玩臂的奖励）尚不清楚，本文旨在解决这一开放问题。

Method: 理论分析与下界构造。针对随机但臂间相关奖励环境，构造下界证明全反馈结果无法推广；针对随机i.i.d.奖励环境，设计算法实现上界并建立匹配的下界。

Result: 在臂间相关的随机奖励（及 adversarial环境）中，任何算法遗憾至少为Ω(√(T-k))；在随机i.i.d.奖励中，可实现Õ(min{T/k, √(T-k)})的遗憾，且该结果在log因子内最优。

Conclusion: 完整刻画了bandit-feedback模型下最佳动作查询带来的收益，揭示了不同奖励结构下查询能力的本质局限。

Abstract: We study \emph{multi-armed bandits} (MABs) augmented with \emph{best-action queries}, in which the learner may additionally query an oracle that reveals the best arm in the current round. This setting was recently characterized by Russo et al. [2024] in the \emph{full-feedback} model, where the learner observes the rewards of all arms after each round. They show that, in both \emph{stochastic} and \emph{adversarial} environments, $k$ best-action queries reduce the optimal $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})$ regret to $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\min\{T/k,\sqrt{T}\})$. Whether this improvement extends to the more realistic \emph{bandit-feedback} model -- where the learner observes only the reward of the played arm -- was left as an open problem. We fully resolve this question. When rewards are stochastic but correlated among arms, we show that the full-feedback result does not carry over: any algorithm must incur regret at least $Ω(\sqrt{T-k})$. This lower bound directly extends to adversarial environments. On the positive side, we show that $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\min\{T/k,\sqrt{T-k}\})$ regret is still achievable when rewards are stochastic and i.i.d., and establish a matching lower bound, up to logarithmic factors. Together, these results provide a complete characterization of the benefits of \emph{best-action queries} in the \emph{bandit-feedback} model.

</details>


### [42] [UMEDA: Unified Multi-modal Efficient Data Fusion for Privacy-Preserving Graph Federated Learning via Spectral-Gated Attention and Diffusion-Based Operator Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08288)
*Shih-Yu Lai,Hirozumi Yamaguchi,Shang-Tse Chen,Yu-Lun Liu,Bing-Yu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UMEDA提出了一种图联邦学习框架，通过连续积分算子与谱信号处理聚合异构传感器（Wi-Fi、LiDAR等）的跨模态数据，结合各向异性差分隐私机制，在不依赖节点对应关系的前提下实现模态对齐与隐私保护，在MM-Fi和RELI11D基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在设备无关定位中面临三大挑战：客户端传感器模态与分辨率异构、数据分布漂移、以及隐私噪声破坏定位所需的结构信号。现有联邦聚合方法无法有效处理不同模态间的底层表示不对齐问题。

Result: 在MM-Fi和RELI11D分布外基准上，UMEDA在准确率、收敛速度和通信效率方面均优于最先进的联邦基线，尤其在高模态异质性和严格隐私预算条件下表现突出。

Conclusion: 通过谱域聚合与各向异性隐私噪声注入，UMEDA有效解决了联邦异构传感器定位中的模态不对齐与隐私-效用权衡问题，为跨模态联邦学习提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Device-free localization trains models from heterogeneous wireless and visual sensors (e.g., Wi-Fi, LiDAR) distributed across edge devices. Federated learning offers a privacy-respecting framework, but is brittle when clients differ in sensor modality and resolution, when their data distributions drift, and when privacy noise destroys the structural signal needed for localization. We propose UMEDA, a graph federated learning framework in which clients form nodes of a global graph that share a continuous integral operator, and aggregation is reformulated as spectral signal processing on this operator. Each client encodes its local sensors with a linear-attention layer whose kernel spectrum is low-rank filtered, suppressing modality-specific residuals so clients with different sensors align in a common low-rank subspace. The server then aggregates client updates via a diffusion model over the kernel's spectral coefficients, treating updates as discretizations of a shared operator rather than topology-bound weights -- this absorbs varying graph sizes and missing modalities without node-wise correspondence. To balance privacy and utility, we add an anisotropic differential-privacy mechanism that projects noise preferentially into the null space of the signal subspace, preserving dominant eigendirections while ensuring formal $(ε, δ)$-DP under gradient clipping. On MM-Fi and the RELI11D out-of-distribution benchmark, UMEDA outperforms state-of-the-art federated baselines in accuracy, convergence, and communication efficiency, particularly under high modality heterogeneity and tight privacy budgets.

</details>


### [43] [What If We Let Forecasting Forget? A Sparse Bottleneck for Cross-Variable Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08289)
*Fan Zhang,Shiming Fan,Hua Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MS-FLOW提出了一种稀疏瓶颈框架，通过选择性稀疏路由替代全连接通信，以容量受限的信息流显式建模变量间依赖，在12个真实基准上取得最先进预测精度，实现了从"更多交互"到"更有效交互"的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法增强了跨通道交互但难以可靠捕获变量间依赖，因为真实数据中的依赖往往是状态依赖且含噪声的，密集交互会放大虚假相关并导致表示过平滑，产生不可靠预测。

Method: 提出MS-FLOW稀疏瓶颈框架，用选择性稀疏路由替代全连接通信，在严格通信预算下仅保留少数关键依赖路径注入跨变量信号，抑制冗余连接和虚假相关传播。

Result: 在12个真实世界基准上取得最先进预测精度，学习到更可靠的多变量相关性和更少但更可靠的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 多变量时序预测应从追求"更多交互"转向"更有效交互"，稀疏化瓶颈设计能有效抑制虚假相关并提升预测可靠性。

Abstract: Multivariate time series forecasting is critical in many real-world systems, and thus modeling cross-channel dependencies is essential. Although existing methods improve overall accuracy by enhancing representations and cross-channel interactions, it remains challenging to reliably capture inter-variable dependencies under specific conditions. We observe that dependencies in real data are often state-dependent and noisy; in such cases, dense interactions can amplify spurious correlations and lead to representation over-smoothing, which may yield unreliable predictions in certain scenarios. Motivated by this, we propose MS-FLOW, a sparse-bottleneck framework that explicitly models inter-variable interaction as capacity-limited information flow. Specifically, MS-FLOW replaces fully connected communication with selective sparse routing, retaining only a few critical dependency paths and injecting cross-variable signals under a strict communication budget, thereby suppressing redundant connections and spurious-correlation propagation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MS-FLOW learns more reliable multivariate correlations, achieving state-of-the-art forecasting accuracy on 12 real-world benchmarks while producing fewer yet more reliable dependencies, shifting multivariate forecasting from "more interaction" to "more effective interaction".

</details>

*Kalana Kalupahana,Francesco Emanuele Stradi,Matteo Castiglioni,Alberto Marchesi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个在鲁棒动态定价中解耦腐败C和时间T依赖的遗憾保证，在已知C时达到O(C+log T)，未知时达到O(C+log² T)，解决了Gupta等人[2025]留下的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒动态定价的遗憾界将腐败C与时间T耦合（如O(C log log T)），无法独立刻画两者对算法性能的影响，这成为一个开放问题。解耦C和T的依赖关系能更清晰地理解腐败对在线学习算法的影响。

Method: 提出了一种鲁棒二叉搜索（robust binary search）变体，通过设计对至多C轮腐败具有鲁棒性的价格搜索策略，实现了遗憾与C的线性依赖而非乘积依赖。对于已知C和未知C两种情况分别设计了算法。

Result: 当腐败C已知时，遗憾界为O(C + log T)；当腐败C未知时，遗憾界为O(C + log² T)。两者均实现了腐败项C与时间项T的加法解耦，显著优于此前O(C log log T)的耦合界。

Conclusion: 成功解决了动态定价中鲁棒遗憾解耦的开放问题，证明了腐败的影响可以仅以加法项而非乘法项的形式出现在遗憾界中，为鲁棒在线学习提供了更精细的理论理解。

Abstract: We design the first regret guarantees for robust dynamic pricing that decouple the dependence on the corruption $C$ and the time horizon $T$. In dynamic pricing, a seller with unlimited supply of a good interacts with a stream of buyers over \( T \) rounds, with the goal of maximizing revenue. At each round $t$, the seller posts a price $p_t$, and the buyer purchases the good only if their unknown valuation $v^\star$ exceeds this price. The seller observes only the binary feedback $\mathbb{I} \left\{ p_t \leq v^\star \right\}$, indicating whether a sale occurred. In the \emph{robust} pricing setting, a malicious adversary is allowed to corrupt this feedback in at most $C$ rounds. Even if the learner knows the corruption $C$, the best known regret bound is $\mathcal{O}(C\log\log T)$ by Gupta et al. [2025]. This leaves as an open problem to ``decouple'' the dependence on $C$ and $T$. In this work, we resolve this open problem. In particular, we develop a robust variant of binary search that achieves regret $\mathcal{O}(C+\log T)$ when the corruption $C$ is known and $\mathcal{O}(C+\log^2 T)$ when the corruption is unknown.

</details>

*Gleb Molodtsov,Alexander Miasnikov,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Hi-MoE提出了一种分层分组混合专家框架，通过组间负载均衡和组内专家特化两个耦合层级解决MoE路由器的负载均衡与专家特化之间的根本权衡，在NLP和视觉基准上取得一致改进，在58B token预训练中相比OLMoE-7B实现5.6%困惑度降低和40%专家平衡提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE路由器存在根本性权衡：强负载均衡会抑制专家特化，而激进的多样性会导致路由坍缩。需要一种新框架来同时实现负载均衡和稳定的专家特化。

Method: 提出Hi-MoE分组混合专家框架，将路由控制分解为两个耦合层级：(i) 组间均衡——强制跨专家组的流量公平分配；(ii) 组内特化——促进组内专家的互补行为并防止组内坍缩。通过层次化目标重塑路由器，实现稳定特化并缓解坍缩。

Result: 在NLP和视觉基准上一致优于近期稀疏路由和分组MoE基线；在58B token大规模预训练中，Hi-MoE-7B相比OLMoE-7B实现5.6%困惑度降低和40%专家平衡提升；通过模型规模、专家数量的缩放研究及针对性消融实验验证了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Hi-MoE通过分层分组机制有效解耦了负载均衡与专家特化的矛盾，为MoE模型提供了一种更稳定、更高效的扩展方案。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models scale capacity by routing each token to a small subset of experts. However, their routers exhibit a fundamental trade-off: strong load balancing can suppress expert specialization, while aggressive diversity often causes routing collapse. We propose Hi-MoE, a grouped MoE framework that decomposes routing control into two coupled levels: (i) inter-group balancing that enforces fair traffic across expert groups, and (ii) intra-group specialization that promotes complementary expert behaviors while preventing within-group collapse. Our analysis provides a principled explanation of how our hierarchical objectives reshape the router, thereby promoting stable specialization and mitigating collapse. We observe consistent improvements over recent sparse-routing and grouped-MoE baselines across NLP and vision benchmarks, and confirm robustness via scaling studies (model size, expert count) and targeted ablations. In large-scale pre-training on 58B tokens, Hi-MoE-7B achieves a 5.6% perplexity reduction and a 40% improvement in expert balance over OLMoE-7B across diverse evaluation domains.

</details>


### [46] [In-Context Fixation: When Demonstrated Labels Override Semantics in Few-Shot Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08295)
*Ming Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现上下文学习（ICL）中，即使标签语义有效，只要标签内容同质化（homogeneous），模型准确率就会崩溃至≤12%。核心机制是模型将标签位置中的token视为完备的答案词汇表，表现出对演示集合的词汇绑定（set-level fixation），而非基于潜在概念的贝叶斯推理。该效应在多token表述和四分类任务中泛化，并通过激活修补定位到Pythia-1B的第7层回路（秩2/560，99.8百分位），跨架构实验（Llama-3.2-1B的logit透镜）验证了"编码-覆盖"轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战现有ICL理论（如贝叶斯潜在概念模型Xie et al., 2022），探究标签同质性对ICL性能的影响机制，揭示模型在ICL中的真实行为模式。

Method: 在六种模型（Pythia、Llama、Qwen，0.8B-8B）和四种任务上进行系统性实验，设计同质/异质标签对照、无意义token集合固定分析、多token表述分解（格式层与内容层）、激活修补（activation patching）与logit透镜等机制分析方法。

Result: 同质标签导致准确率≤12%；集合固定效应：42-67%概率分配给演示集合token，而P(dog)<0.2%；四分类准确率0%；激活修补恢复98.4%的差距，定位到第7层回路（99.8百分位）；跨架构验证了"编码-覆盖"轨迹。

Conclusion: ICL的输出本质上是受约束的词汇检索过程（vocabulary retrieval），模型将其输出绑定到演示中出现的token集合，与语义合理性无关。这一发现对ICL的理论理解和实际应用（如提示工程）具有重要启示。

Abstract: While random demonstration labels barely hurt in-context learning (Min et al., 2022), we show that homogeneous labels--even semantically valid ones--collapse accuracy to <=12% across six models (Pythia, Llama, Qwen; 0.8B--8B) and four tasks. The trigger is label-slot content: the model treats tokens occupying the label position as an exhaustive answer vocabulary, with homogeneity as the maximally collapsed case. A novel set-level fixation finding confirms this: when demonstrations carry varied nonsense tokens from {foo,bar,vex,nit,orb}, the model places 42--67% of probability on the demonstrated set while P(dog) remains below 0.2%. This is inconsistent with latent-concept Bayesian accounts (Xie et al., 2022) and reveals that ICL output is constrained vocabulary retrieval--the model binds its output to the demonstrated token inventory regardless of semantic plausibility. The effect generalizes to 4-way classification (0% accuracy across three models, 1B--8B) and multi-token verbalizers ("very positive"), where we decompose fixation into format-level (template adoption) and content-level (polarity override) components that are experimentally dissociable. Mechanistically, per-item paired activation patching on Pythia-1B recovers 98.4% of the gap (95% CI [84%, 112%]), localizing fixation to a layer-7-centered circuit (rank 2/560, 99.8th percentile; 4-fold CV mean 103%). Cross-architecture logit lens on Llama-3.2-1B replicates the encode-then-override trajectory with causal confirmation (top-5 layers: 89% recovery).

</details>


### [47] [SGC-RML: A reliable and interpretable longitudinal assessment for PD in real-world DNS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08302)
*Wenbin Wei,Ruixiang Gao,Suyuan Yao,Xuanzhen Zhao,Cheng Huang,Hen-Wei Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SGC-RML框架，通过8维症状节点空间（含1个可靠性节点）统一异构模态数据，结合不确定性估计、共形校准和选择性决策路由，实现对帕金森病的可校准、可审计、症状可解释的回顾性纵向评估，并在五个真实数据集上取得优异结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界帕金森病数字评估面临模态异构、跨设备偏差和标签不完整等挑战，现有方法仅关注平均预测性能，缺乏可靠性感知机制（如何时可靠、何时拒绝、何时重测、预测基于哪些症状维度）。

Method: 将语音、步态、可穿戴运动、移动任务和临床变量映射到共享的8维症状节点空间（7个临床症状节点+1个reliability_state辅助节点），通过症状图谱统一运动和非运动表征，联合引入不确定性估计、共形校准和选择性决策路由，实现预测、拒绝或建议重测。

Result: PPMI上MAE 4.579/R² 0.772，mPower上AUC 0.953，PADS上AUC 0.825；无泄漏时间锚定下仅5个受试者特定锚点使UCI从不可预测（运动MAE 8.38, CCC 0.02）变为校准纵向评估（运动MAE 3.24, CCC 0.756），共形覆盖率达0.80目标；Daphnet LOSO协议下F1 0.803/AUC 0.872。

Conclusion: SGC-RML为不完整多模态条件下的帕金森病回顾性纵向评估提供了统一范式，实现了准确、可校准、可审计且症状可解释的评估。

Abstract: Real-world digital Parkinson's disease assessment faces challenges such as heterogeneous modalities, cross-device bias, and incomplete labeling. Existing methods often focus on average predictive performance, lacking the reliability mechanisms needed for retrospective reliability-aware assessment - namely, determining when the model is reliable, when to reject an assessment, when to retest, and from which symptom dimensions the predictions are based. This paper proposes SGC-RML, which maps speech, gait, wearable motion, mobility tasks, and clinical variables to a shared 8-dimensional symptom node space (7 clinical symptom nodes and 1 reliability_state auxiliary node), unifying motor and non-motor representations through a symptom atlas. By jointly introducing uncertainty estimation, conformal calibration, and selective decision routing, the model can not only predict symptoms and severity but also reject assessments or suggest retests when evidence is insufficient. We validate this framework on five real-world PD datasets, covering classification, regression, event detection, and longitudinal severity prediction. Experiments show that SGC-RML achieves an MAE of 4.579 / R^2 of 0.772 on PPMI, an AUC of 0.953 on mPower, and an AUC of 0.825 on PADS. Under leak-free temporal anchoring, as few as 5 subject-specific anchors transform UCI from an essentially non-predictive subject-independent setting (motor MAE 8.38, CCC 0.02) into a calibrated longitudinal assessment (motor MAE 3.24, CCC 0.756) with split-conformal coverage held at the 0.80 target. Under the Daphnet LOSO protocol, it achieves an F1 of 0.803 / AUC of 0.872. These results demonstrate that SGC-RML provides a unified paradigm for accurate, calibrated, auditable, and symptom-interpretable retrospective longitudinal assessment of PD under incomplete multimodal conditions.

</details>


### [48] [LLMSYS-HPOBench: Hyperparameter Optimization Benchmark Suite for Real-World LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08305)
*Siyu Wu,Yulong Ye,Zezhen Xiang,Pengzhou Chen,Gangda Xiong,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LLMSYS-HPOBench，首个针对真实世界大语言模型系统的超参数优化基准套件，包含364,450个超参数配置、12-23维超参数空间、3-5维保真度因子、932个设置、3-9个推理目标指标和2-10个成本指标，旨在为AutoML社区提供LLM系统HPO研究的演化平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HPO基准未能充分捕捉LLM系统的独特挑战：AI与非AI组件的复合超参数空间、保真度因子的丰富非线性影响、以及测量超参数配置的多样化成本。

Method: 通过实际运行LLM系统收集超参数配置的推理目标值，构建包含高维度、多保真度因子、多目标/成本指标的基准数据集，并以实时更新的方式发布。

Result: LLMSYS-HPOBench包含364,450个超参数配置，维度12-23，3-5维保真度因子（共932个设置），3-9个推理目标指标，2-10个成本指标，并附带日志数据。

Conclusion: 该基准不仅用于重新验证现有HPO算法在LLM系统上的表现，更重要的是为AutoML社区提供探索新研究方向（如复合空间优化、多保真度HPO、成本感知优化等）的演化平台。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) systems have been the frontier of AI in many application domains, leading to new challenges and opportunities for hyperparameter optimization (HPO) for the AutoML community. However, this type of system exhibits an unprecedented compound space of hyperparameter configuration from both the AI and non-AI components; rich and nonlinear implications from the fidelity factors; and diverse costs of measuring hyperparameter configurations, none of which have been fully captured in existing benchmarks. This paper presents the first (live) benchmark suite and datasets for HPO of real-world LLM systems, dubbed LLMSYS-HPOBench, covering data related to the inference objective values of hyperparameter configurations profiled from running the LLM systems. Currently, LLMSYS-HPOBench contains 364,450 hyperparameter configurations with a dimensionality of 12-23, 3-5 dimensions of fidelity factor leading to 932 settings, 3-9 inference objective metrics, and 2-10 cost metrics, together with generated logs from measuring the LLM systems. What we seek to advocate is not only a revalidation of the existing HPO algorithms over the frontier LLM systems, but also to provide an evolving platform for the AutoML community to explore new directions of research in this regard. The benchmark suite has been made available at: https://github.com/ideas-labo/llmsys-hpobench

</details>


### [49] [Revitalizing the Beginning: Avoiding Storage Dependency for Model Merging in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08311)
*Xi Wang,Cheng Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Trajectory Regularized Merging (TRM)框架，将模型合并重新定义为增强轨迹子空间内的优化过程，通过任务对齐、预测一致性和梯度响应三个协同目标，解决了持续学习场景下现有模型合并方法存在的任务误差累积和梯度消失问题，实现了多任务SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并有望将专业模型集成为统一的多任务模型，但持续学习中的存储限制与多样性知识保留之间存在冲突。现有方法在持续学习约束下存在两个关键问题：(1)全局对齐导致连续数据流中任务特定误差的累积与放大；(2)后续任务初始阶段的梯度消失使优化停滞，导致合并模型在下一训练阶段开始时处于次优状态。

Method: 提出Trajectory Regularized Merging (TRM)框架，将合并阶段重构为增强轨迹子空间内的优化过程，包含三个协同目标：任务对齐（保持历史稳定性）、预测一致性（确保输出连贯性）和梯度响应（重新激活优化动力学）。

Result: 在多个基准测试上实现了state-of-the-art性能。

Conclusion: TRM通过将合并重新定义为轨迹子空间中的多目标优化，有效解决了持续学习场景下模型合并的误差累积和优化停滞问题，为多任务模型集成提供了新的方法论框架。

Abstract: Model merging provides a compelling paradigm for integrating specialized expertise into a unified multi-task model, a goal that aligns naturally with the sequential knowledge acquisition in continual learning (CL). However, the requirement for preserving diverse forms of previous knowledge conflicts with the storage limitations inherent to CL. In this paper, we systematically analyze existing model merging methods under the constraints of CL. We find that current methods prioritize global alignment, which often leads to the accumulation and amplification of task-specific errors within the continuous data stream; and the vanishing gradients at the onset of subsequent tasks frequently cause optimization to stagnate. These leave the merged model in a suboptimal state at the beginning of the next training phase. To address these challenges, we propose Trajectory Regularized Merging (TRM), a framework that reformulates the merging phase as an optimization process within an augmented trajectory subspace. Our framework integrates three synergistic objectives including task alignment, prediction consistency, and gradient responsiveness to concurrently preserve merged model's historical stability and re-activate optimization dynamics. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple benchmarks.

</details>


### [50] [FlashSVD v1.5: Making Low-Rank Transformers Inference Actually Fast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08314)
*Wenhao Wu,Zishan Shao,Kangning Cui,Jinhee Kim,Yixiao Wang,Hancheng Ye,Danyang Zhuo,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlashSVD v1.5通过运行时协同设计（包括阶段特定内核、密集KV解码、打包MLP执行和逐层CUDA图重放），解决了SVD低秩压缩在LLM推理中理论FLOPs减少与实际加速不匹配的问题，在解码和服务端到端场景下分别实现最高2.55倍和2.39倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SVD低秩压缩虽然减少了transformer参数和名义FLOPs，但这些节省在实际LLM服务加速中效果不佳，原因是分解后的检查点碎片化了执行路径，且预填充和自回归解码阶段的运行时开销差异显著。

Method: 将多种SVD压缩家族映射到统一的分解表示，结合阶段特定核函数、密集KV解码、打包MLP执行和逐层CUDA图重放，将低秩服务路径重组为轻量运行时。

Result: 在解码场景下最高2.55倍加速，端到端场景下最高2.39倍加速；在多种SVD压缩家族上平均解码加速1.48倍，平均端到端加速1.44倍。

Conclusion: 实际的低秩加速需要运行时协同设计，而非仅仅依赖压缩算法本身。

Abstract: SVD-based Low-rank compression reduces transformer parameters and nominal FLOPs, but these savings often translate poorly into real LLM serving speedups. We show that this gap is largely a runtime problem: factorized checkpoints fragment execution paths, and the resulting overhead differs substantially between prefill and autoregressive decode. We present FlashSVD v1.5, a unified inference runtime for serving SVD-compressed transformers. FlashSVD v1.5 maps diverse public SVD compression families to a common factorized representation and combines phase-specific kernels with dense-KV decode, packed MLP execution, and per-layer CUDA-graph replay to reorganize the low-rank serving path into a thin runtime. Across representative decoder-serving settings, FlashSVD v1.5 achieves up to 2.55x decode and 2.39x end-to-end speedup, and it attains 1.48x average decode and 1.44x average end-to-end speedup across multiple popular SVD compression families. These results suggest that practical low-rank acceleration requires runtime co-design, not compression algorithms alone. Our code is available at: https://github.com/Zishan-Shao/FlashSVD.

</details>


### [51] [Reflective Prompted Policy Optimization: Trajectory-Grounded Revision and Salience Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08315)
*Rahaf Abu Hara,Vaibbhav Murarri,Claudio Zito*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出R2PO框架，将轨迹级行为证据作为上下文增强标量奖励反馈，通过分离全局搜索与行为修正、使用选择过滤高方差编辑，使20B LLM在10个环境中实现最高平均奖励、更快收敛和更稳定训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的策略优化器仅依赖标量奖励反馈（如策略得分为0.45），无法获取轨迹层面的行为证据（如智能体陷入循环、第三步掉入坑中、19/20次成功但一次灾难性失败），导致诊断不精确、学习效率低。

Method: R2PO采用两阶段框架：Search-LLM提出候选策略参数，环境执行后Critic-LLM检查轨迹并提出基于状态-动作-奖励的针对性修正。关键设计包括：(1) 全局搜索与行为修正分离；(2) 选择机制过滤高方差编辑；(3) 通过聚合轨迹统计、中位数轨迹选择和修正规则缓解显著性偏差。

Result: 在10个环境中，R2PO使用20B开源模型达到最高平均最佳奖励，在约500个回合内达到CartPole近最优性能，训练稳定性远超深度RL和现有LLM方法。显著性偏差解释了CartPole上76.6%的性能退化。

Conclusion: 将轨迹视为上下文中的一等证据而非简化为标量回报，能够改变即使较小LLM搜索策略空间的方式，使其学习更快、诊断更精确、更可靠地改进外部控制器。

Abstract: Existing LLM-based policy optimizers see only scalar rewards: that a policy scored 0.45, but not whether the agent got stuck in a loop, fell into a hole on the third step, or performed well on 19 out of 20 rollouts and failed catastrophically on one. We propose Reflective Prompted Policy Optimization (R2PO), a two-stage LLM framework for policy search over compact policy classes that augments scalar reward feedback with trajectory-level behavioral evidence. A Search-LLM proposes candidate policy parameters; the environment executes them; a Critic-LLM inspects the resulting rollouts and proposes targeted revisions grounded in observed states, actions, and rewards. Across ten environments, ablations show R2PO's gains require separating global search from behavior-grounded revision and using selection to filter high-variance edits. We further identify a dominant failure mode, salience bias: when presented with multiple rollouts, the Critic-LLM fixates on improving a single failure even when most trajectories succeed. In a three-trajectory variant where the Critic-LLM sees the best, worst, and median rollout, this behavior explains 76.6% of regressions on CartPole. R2PO mitigates this by reasoning over aggregate rollout statistics, median-trajectory selection, and a revision rule. Using a 20B open-weight model, R2PO achieves the highest mean best reward across all ten environments, reaches near-optimal performance substantially earlier (e.g., near-maximum CartPole reward within ~500 episodes), and trains far more stably than both deep RL and prior LLM-based methods. These results show that treating trajectories as first-class in-context evidence, rather than artifacts reduced to scalar returns, changes how even comparatively small LLMs search over policy spaces, enabling them to learn faster, diagnose more precisely, and reliably improve external controllers.

</details>


### [52] [RDKV: Rate-Distortion Bit Allocation for Joint Eviction and Quantization of the KV Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08317)
*Junkai Zhang,Hang Guo,Luca Benini,Yawei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将KV缓存压缩问题形式化为率失真优化问题，提出联合优化逐出和量化两种策略的方法RDKV，通过反向注水算法为每个token/channel分配比特宽度，在LongBench等基准上以2.48%的缓存保留率恢复97.81%的全精度准确率，实现4.5倍解码加速和1.9倍峰值内存降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM长上下文推理时KV缓存随序列长度线性增长，需从片外HBM重复读取导致内存瓶颈。现有逐出和量化方法相互孤立，缺乏联合优化视角。

Method: 将KV缓存压缩建模为率失真问题，推导每个token/channel对注意力计算引入的失真权重，基于反向注水算法在预填充阶段后一次性分配从全精度到零比特的位宽。

Result: 在LongBench、RULER、InfiniteBench上平均超越最佳基线9.1%；LongBench上以2.48%缓存保留率恢复97.81%全精度准确率；128K上下文下相比FlashAttention-2实现4.5倍解码加速和1.9倍峰值内存降低。

Conclusion: 联合优化逐出与量化能显著提升KV缓存压缩效率，率失真框架为缓存压缩提供了统一的理论基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong performance across diverse tasks, but their inference with long input contexts is bottlenecked by memory size and bandwidth. The Key-Value (KV) cache size grows linearly with sequence length and needs to be re-read from off-chip high-bandwidth memory (HBM) to on-chip memory at every decoding step, resulting in memory-bound inference. Existing methods reduce the cache by either eviction or quantization, but typically treat the two in isolation. In this paper, we cast KV cache compression as a rate-distortion problem, under which eviction and quantization are two end-points of the same bit allocation scheme. This exposes the need to optimize them jointly, motivating our method, RDKV (Rate-Distortion KV cache compression). RDKV derives the weight of each token or channel from the distortion that compression induces on the attention computation. Based on these weights, it assigns each token or channel a bit-width ranging from full precision down to zero bits guided by reverse water-filling, applied once after the prefilling stage. Experiments on LongBench, RULER, and InfiniteBench show that RDKV outperforms the best evaluated baseline by 9.1% on average. On LongBench it recovers 97.81% of full-cache accuracy with only 2.48% cache retention. Compared with full-cache FlashAttention-2 decoding, it achieves 4.5x decode speedup and 1.9x peak memory reduction with 128K context length, while maintaining comparable performance.

</details>


### [53] [When Attention Beats Fourier: Multi-Scale Transformers for PDE Solving on Irregular Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08318)
*Brandon Yee,Pairie Koh,Jack Rodriguez,Mihir Tekal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多尺度注意力Transformer（MSAT）用于求解偏微分方程，在复杂几何问题上相比FNO提升3.7倍性能，同时推理速度比Mamba-NO快约3553倍，并给出了基于边界复杂度的架构选择理论准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习求解PDE的方法（如PINNs、神经算子、状态空间模型）在架构选择上缺乏系统性的指导原则，特别是Transformer架构与傅里叶域神经算子之间的性能对比尚不清晰，需要建立理论基础来指导特定PDE问题应选用何种架构。

Method: 提出MSAT架构，将时空解历史编码为token序列，采用复合监督目标（含可选物理信息正则化项）进行端到端训练。在PINNacle基准套件的5个问题上与9种基线方法进行公平对比，并开展了物理正则化消融实验和近似误差界的理论分析。

Result: MSAT在复杂几何热传导问题Heat2D-CG上达到L2_rel=0.0101（FNO的3.7倍提升），推理时间34秒，Mamba-NO需120812秒。物理先验在扩散主导问题上降低测试误差，但在混沌和回流区域降低泛化性能。

Conclusion: 建立了基于域边界复杂度κ的近似误差界，为架构选择提供了理论依据：扩散主导问题适合引入物理先验，混沌/回流问题则应避免物理先验的误设。

Abstract: We study the problem of \emph{architecture selection} for deep learning models trained to solve partial differential equations (PDEs), asking when transformer-based architectures with learned attention outperform Fourier-domain neural operators. We introduce the \textbf{Multi-Scale Attention Transformer} (\msat{}), a deep learning architecture that encodes spatiotemporal solution histories as token sequences and trains end-to-end via a composite supervised objective with optional physics-informed regularization terms. We conduct a comprehensive empirical evaluation against nine baselines -- including physics-informed neural networks (PINNs), neural operators (FNO, DeepONet, GNOT), and state-space models (Mamba-NO) -- across five benchmark problems from the PINNacle suite, using identical train/test splits and reference data for all methods. \msat{} achieves state-of-the-art generalization on complex geometry problems ($L^2_\mathrm{rel} = 0.0101$ on Heat2D-CG, a $3.7\times$ improvement over FNO) at $34\,\mathrm{s}$ total inference vs.\ $120{,}812\,\mathrm{s}$ for Mamba-NO. Ablation studies over the physics regularization component reveal a precise inductive bias tradeoff: physics priors reduce test error on diffusion-dominated problems but degrade generalization on chaotic and recirculating-flow regimes, directly characterizing the prior misspecification boundary. Approximation error bounds as a function of domain boundary complexity $κ$ provide a theoretical basis for these empirical findings and a principled rule for architecture selection.

</details>


### [54] [LLM Wardens: Mitigating Adversarial Persuasion with Third-Party Conversational Oversight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08321)
*Lennart Wachowiak,Scott D. Blain,David Williams-King,Samuele Marro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了对抗性LLM在决策场景中对用户的操纵能力，并提出了一种"典狱长"模型（次级LLM）来实时监控交互并发出非约束性建议。实验表明，对抗性LLM成功操纵用户决策的比例为65.4%，加入典狱长后降至30.4%；在模拟基准测试中，操纵成功率从34.7%降至12.3%，且即使典狱长模型弱于被监督的对手也能提供有效保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的劝说能力日益增强，引发了对用户如何免受操纵的担忧，因此需要研究对抗性操纵的防御机制。

Method: 通过预注册用户研究（N=120，涵盖4个决策场景）评估对抗性LLM的操纵成功率；引入"典狱长"次级LLM实时监控交互痕迹并发出非约束性私人建议；构建COAX-Bench模拟基准（14个决策场景，16,212次多智能体交互）进行大规模评估。

Result: 对抗性LLM在用户研究中操纵成功率为65.4%，加入典狱长后降至30.4%（真实交互成功率仅降低8.6个百分点）；COAX-Bench中对抗性LLM成功率为34.7%，典狱长将其降至12.3%；弱典狱长模型也能提供有效保护。

Conclusion: 典狱长模型能有效降低对抗性LLM的操纵成功率，为更强模型的扩展监督提供了可行路径。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly capable of persuasion, which raises the question of how to protect users against manipulation. In a preregistered user study (N=120) across four decision-making scenarios, we find that an adversarial LLM with a hidden goal succeeds in steering users' decisions 65.4% of the time. We then introduce a "warden" model: a secondary LLM that monitors the human-AI interaction trace in real time and issues non-binding, private advisories to the user when it detects manipulation. Adding a warden more than halves the adversary's success rate to 30.4%, with a much smaller (8.6 percentage points) reduction for genuine interactions. To probe the mechanism behind these results, we release COAX-Bench, a simulation benchmark spanning 14 decision-making scenarios, including hiring, voting, and file access. Across 16,212 simulated multi-agent interactions, capable adversarial LLMs achieve their hidden goals in 34.7% of cases, which warden models reduce to 12.3%. Notably, even warden models substantially weaker than the adversary they oversee provide meaningful protection, suggesting a path for scalable oversight of more capable models.

</details>


### [55] [SDG-MoE: Signed Debate Graph Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08322)
*Stepan Kulibaba,Kirill Labzin,Artem Dzhalilov,Roman Pakhomov,Oleg Svidchenko,Alexander Gansnikov,Aleksei Shpilman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SDG-MoE，一种通过支持图与批判图实现活跃专家间有符号消息传递与辩论式迭代交互的稀疏MoE架构，在多个语言建模基准上显著优于传统MoE和未签名图基线，且仅增加低阶计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏MoE架构中，每个token被路由到的专家彼此独立处理，其输出仅通过加权求和聚合，专家间缺乏直接通信。本文旨在探索是否让活跃专家之间进行结构化交互可以提升模型性能。

Method: 提出SDG-MoE，包含三个核心组件：(i) 两个学习到的交互矩阵——支持图A⁺和批判图A⁻，分别捕获专家间的增强性影响和修正性影响；(ii) 有符号消息传递步骤，在聚合前更新专家表示；(iii) 基于分歧门控的Friedkin-Johnsen式锚定机制，控制辩论强度并防止专家漂移。该机制使交互强度随分歧程度自适应调节，同时保持专家特化性。

Result: 在三次种子预训练实验中，SDG-MoE的验证困惑度比未签名图通信基线和传统MoE分别提升，超过最强基线19.8%；在WikiText-103、C4和Paloma上取得最佳外部困惑度。

Conclusion: 活跃专家间的结构化有符号辩论交互能够有效提升MoE模型性能，且通过理论分析证明了稳定性条件，表明该方法仅增加低阶计算开销，是一种高效可行的改进方向。

Abstract: Sparse MoE models achieve a good balance between capacity and compute by routing each token to a small subset of experts. However, in most MoE architectures, once a token is routed, the selected experts process it independently and their outputs are combined via a weighted sum. This leaves open whether enabling communication among them could improve performance. While prior work has raised this question, direct interaction among the active routed experts remains underexplored. In this paper, we propose SDG-MoE (Signed Debate Graph Mixture-of-Experts), a novel architecture that adds a lightweight, iterative deliberation step before final aggregation. SDG-MoE introduces three components: (i) two learned interaction matrices over the active experts, a support graph $A^+$ and a critique graph $A^-$, capturing reinforcing and corrective influences; (ii) a signed message-passing step that updates expert representations before aggregation; and (iii) a disagreement-gated Friedkin-Johnsen-style anchoring that controls deliberation strength while preventing expert drift. Together, these enable a structured deliberation process where interaction strength scales with disagreement and specialization is preserved. We also provide a theoretical analysis establishing stability conditions on expert states and showing that deliberation adds only low-order overhead over the active set. In controlled three-seed pretraining experiments, SDG-MoE improves validation perplexity over both an unsigned graph communication baseline and vanilla MoE, outperforming the strongest baseline by 19.8%, and gives the best external perplexity on WikiText-103, C4, and Paloma among the compared systems.

</details>


### [56] [The Reciprocity Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08323)
*Yue Lin,Pascal Poupart,Shuhui Zhu,Dan Qiao,Wenhao Li,Yuan Liu,Hongyuan Zha,Baoxiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了互惠梯度方法，通过将奖励梯度反向传播经过对手策略的私有估计器，解决策略交互中Agent的影响力归因问题，实现了无需内在奖励的联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略交互中Agent的任何行动或信号都会沿着组合分支路径重塑众多第三方的声誉，然后反馈到自身未来奖励，存在影响力归因这一核心优化难题，Agent必须同时考虑所有间接渠道来选择行动。

Method: 提出互惠梯度（reciprocity gradient），将奖励梯度显式地反向传播通过从公共观测训练的对手策略的私有估计器，梯度通过声誉链本身解析地流动，而非从采样回报中估计，联合优化行动和评估信号，无需内在奖励或奖励塑形。

Result: 该方法恢复出接近最优的上下文敏感策略，而基于采样的基线方法崩溃为恒定输出策略。

Conclusion: 互惠梯度通过解析梯度传播有效解决了策略交互中的影响力归因问题，实现了比采样方法更优的策略学习。

Abstract: Communication is fundamental to sustaining reciprocity and cooperation in strategic interactions. We identify and formulate the influence attribution problem as the central optimization difficulty inherent in such dynamics for a learning agent: any action or signal the agent emits reshapes the reputations of many third parties along combinatorially branching paths before feeding back into its own future rewards, forcing the agent to account for all of these indirect channels at once when choosing every action. To address this, we introduce the reciprocity gradient, which explicitly backpropagates reward gradients through private estimators of opponents' policies trained from public observations. The gradient flows through the reputation chain itself analytically, rather than being estimated from sampled returns. It jointly optimizes actions and evaluative signals without intrinsic rewards or reward shaping. Empirically, the method recovers near-optimal context-sensitive policies, while sample-based baselines collapse into constant-output policies.

</details>


### [57] [Interactive Critique-Revision Training for Reliable Structured LLM Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08327)
*Fei Xu Yu,Zuyuan Zhang,Mahdi Imani,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DPA-GRPO方法，通过生成器-验证器双人博弈和配对反事实动作组的策略优化，在结构化决策任务中实现局部正确、全局一致且可审计的LLM输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM精炼方法依赖启发式辩论、自博弈或LLM生成的监督信号，存在二阶保证问题（即谁来监督监督者），无法确保结构化决策中的局部正确性、全局一致性和可审计性。

Method: 设计双玩家生成器-验证器博弈：生成器输出并可在被质疑时修订；验证器选择沉默或提出安全保证案例（SAC）。通过SAC/no-SAC与KEEP/REVISE决策构建配对反事实动作组，结合角色特定的KL正则化GRPO更新进行训练。

Result: 在TaxCalcBench TY24实验中，DPA-GRPO相较于零样本生成和仅生成器强化学习基线，在Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B上均提升了结构化决策准确率，增加了正确的静默接受，减少了漏检错误，并改善了校准化的修订行为。

Conclusion: DPA-GRPO通过博弈论框架和配对动作优化，有效解决了LLM结构化决策中的二阶保证问题，同时提升了生成器和验证器双方性能。

Abstract: In structured decision-making workflows such as form filling, compliance checking, and maintenance reporting, LLM outputs must be locally correct, globally consistent, and auditable against task-specific rules. Existing refinement methods often rely on heuristic debate, self-play, or LLM-generated supervision, creating a second-order assurance problem. We propose DPA-GRPO (Dual Paired-Action Group-Relative Policy Optimization), a paired-action training method for a two-player generator--verifier game with structured verifier interventions. The generator proposes outputs and may revise them when challenged; the verifier either remains silent or raises a safety assurance case (SAC) containing a claim, argument, and evidence. These SAC/no-SAC and KEEP/REVISE decisions induce paired counterfactual action groups, which DPA-GRPO uses for role-specific KL-regularized GRPO updates. We analyze the unregularized game and show that positive probability on strictly lower-reward intervention or revision actions creates a profitable unilateral deviation. Under standard stochastic-approximation assumptions, DPA-GRPO tracks the corresponding game ODE, whose isolated asymptotically stable limit points are stationary and candidate local equilibria under role-wise local optimality. Experiments on TaxCalcBench TY24 show that DPA-GRPO improves structured decision accuracy over zero-shot generation and generator-only RL baselines across Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B. Training increases correct silent acceptance, reduces missed errors, and improves calibrated revision behavior, indicating gains for both generator and verifier.

</details>


### [58] [CDS4RAG: Cyclic Dual-Sequential Hyperparameter Optimization for RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08333)
*Pengzhou Chen,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CDS4RAG框架，通过循环双序列优化方法对RAG系统的检索器和生成器全部超参数进行联合优化，在21/24个案例中提升基础算法性能，相比SOTA方法最高提升1.54倍生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统对检索器和生成器的超参数高度敏感，但由于复杂交互和高昂评估成本，使用给定查询优化这些超参数是一项挑战。现有算法将RAG视为黑盒或仅优化部分超参数，导致收敛缓慢且效果不佳。

Method: 提出循环双序列优化框架CDS4RAG，区分检索器和生成器超参数并循环交替优化；在优化生成器时引入细粒度的周期内预算分配和跨周期种子播种加速优化；该框架是算法无关的，可与多种通用算法结合。

Result: 在4个常用基准和2个骨干LLM上，CDS4RAG在21/24个案例中显著提升基础算法性能；在所有案例中优于SOTA算法，生成质量最高提升1.54倍，并具有更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: CDS4RAG通过循环双序列优化范式有效解决了RAG全超参数优化难题，具有算法无关性和显著的性能提升，为RAG系统的自动化调优提供了新思路。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is sensitive to the vast hyperparameters of the retriever and generator, yet optimizing them using given queries is a challenging task due to the complex interactions and expensive evaluation costs. Existing algorithms are ineffective and slow in convergence, since they often treat RAG as a monolithic black box or only optimize partial hyperparameters. In this paper, we propose CDS4RAG, a framework that optimizes the full RAG hyperparameters using given queries via a new cyclic dual-sequential formulation. CDS4RAG is special in the sense that it distinguishes the hyperparameters of the retriever and generator, cyclically optimizing them in turn. Such a paradigm allows us to design fine-grained within-cycle budget provision and expedite the optimization via cross-cycle seeding when optimizing the generator. CDS4RAG is also an algorithm-agnostic framework that can be paired with diverse general algorithms. Through experiments on four common benchmarks and two backbone LLMs, we reveal that CDS4RAG considerably boosts the vanilla algorithms in 21/24 cases while significantly outperforming state-of-the-art algorithms in all cases with up to 1.54x improvements of generation quality and better speedup.

</details>


### [59] [Private Vertical Federated Inference for Time-Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08343)
*Lucas Fenaux,Larris Xie,Aditya Bang,Alex Zhang,Kevin Wilson,Florian Kerschbaum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PPHH-VFL混合架构，将模型头部拆分为高效的明文公共头部和轻量安全的MPC私有头部，结合对抗训练缓解隐私泄露，在最高8600万参数模型上实现比端到端MPC快六个数量级的推理加速，相比VFL+MPC基线在WAN下提速44.4倍、通信成本降低91.2倍，同时提升分类准确率2.50%和回归RMSE 40.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方案中，MPC在时间序列协同推理中提供强隐私保障但开销极大且难以扩展至现代Transformer架构；VFL效率高但存在嵌入层隐私泄露风险，且对整个VFL模型头部施加MPC保护仍过于缓慢且通信开销大。亟需一种兼顾效率与安全性的实用大规模安全推理方案。

Method: 提出PPHH-VFL混合架构，将模型头部拆分为两部分：1）明文公共头部（Public Head），用于高效处理但不泄露敏感信息；2）轻量级MPC私有头部（Private Head），用于安全保留敏感信息流。对公共嵌入施加对抗训练以缓解隐私泄露，私有头部保持轻量化以确保MPC运算高效。

Result: 在最高8600万参数模型上，PPHH-VFL相比端到端MPC推理加速达六个数量级；相比标准VFL+MPC基线，WAN环境下提速44.4倍，通信成本从1.7 GB降至19 MB每批次（降低91.2倍），同时下游分类准确率提升2.50%，回归RMSE改善40.7%。

Conclusion: PPHH-VFL通过巧妙的公私头部拆分设计，在隐私保护、计算效率和模型性能之间取得了优越的平衡，为大规模时间序列数据场景下的实用安全推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Institutions may benefit from collaborative inference on time-series data. In settings where privacy is necessary, multi-party computation (MPC) is a straightforward approach to providing strong guarantees, yet it remains prohibitively expensive and scales poorly with modern transformer architectures. Vertical Federated Learning (VFL) offers efficiency but suffers from privacy leakage at the embedding level, and securing the entire VFL model head via MPC remains prohibitively slow and communication-heavy for larger models. To enable practical, secure inference at scale, we propose "Public/Private Hybrid Head-VFL" (PPHH-VFL). This hybrid architecture splits the model head into an efficient plaintext public head and a secure, lightweight MPC private head. By applying adversarial training to the public embeddings, we mitigate privacy leakage; concurrently, the small private head securely preserves the flow of sensitive information needed for high downstream utility. Empirical evaluations on models ranging up to 86 million parameters demonstrate that PPHH-VFL accelerates inference by up to six orders of magnitude compared to end-to-end MPC. Compared to a standard VFL+MPC baseline, our approach scales significantly better, achieving a speedup of up to 44.4x in WAN and a 91.2x reduction in communication costs (dropping from 1.7 GB to 19 MB per batch), while simultaneously improving downstream classification accuracy by 2.50% and regression RMSE by 40.7%.

</details>


### [60] [What Time Is It? How Data Geometry Makes Time Conditioning Optional for Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08344)
*Alec Helbling,Sebastian Gutierrez Hernandez,Benjamin Hoover,Duen Horng Chau,Parikshit Ram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了盲时间流匹配模型在高维数据中能够有效工作的原因：当数据集中在低维子空间时，时间信息可以从噪声观测中直接识别，使得时间盲区相对于耦合方差渐近可忽略。实证表明，耦合选择比显式时间条件对模型性能影响更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准流匹配模型依赖显式时间条件来消除速度目标的歧义，但近期研究发现无需时间条件也能工作，这与常规认知矛盾。本文旨在解释"盲时间"模型为何在实践中有效，并厘清耦合与时间条件各自的作用。

Method: 分解盲时间流匹配损失，识别出耦合方差（由噪声与数据配对的模糊性引起）和时间盲区差距（忽略时间导致的额外误差）。在尖峰协方差模型下推导出从单次观测中以O(1/√(d-k))速率恢复时间t的闭式估计量，并在CIFAR-10、CelebA-HQ、FFHQ上进行实证验证。

Result: 证明了高维数据的几何特性（数据集中在k维子空间）使得时间可从噪声观测中识别；时间盲区差距相对于耦合方差渐近可忽略；改变耦合对损失与样本质量的影响远大于移除时间条件。

Conclusion: 盲时间流匹配有效的原因是高维数据的低维流形结构使得时间可识别，实际操作中耦合选择是比显式时间条件更重要的性能杠杆。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that models flow matching models can be trained without explicit time conditioning, challenging the standard view that the interpolation time is needed to disambiguate velocity targets. But why should a time-blind model work at all? Decomposing the time-blind flow matching loss, we identify two sources of irreducible error: a coupling variance, which arises from ambiguous velocity targets induced by how noise and data points are paired, and the time-blindness gap, which is the additional error caused by ignoring time. This gap shows that time-blind training is strictly harder than conventional training, reinforcing the puzzle that time-blind models work so well in practice. We resolve this tension by showing that the geometry of high-dimensional data makes time identifiable directly from noisy observations. When data concentrates near a $k$-dimensional subspace, time can be recovered from the statistical structure of noisy interpolants in directions orthogonal to the data; under a spiked-covariance model, this yields a closed-form estimator that recovers $t$ from a single observation $z$ at rate $O(1/\sqrt{d-k})$ for ambient dimension $d$. As a consequence, we prove that the time-blindness gap is asymptotically negligible relative to the coupling variance. We empirically demonstrate our identifiability result on real-world data and show that changing the coupling has a much larger effect on loss and sample quality than removing time conditioning across CIFAR-10, CelebA-HQ, and FFHQ. These results explain why time-blind flow matching works and show that the main practical lever is the choice of coupling, not explicit time conditioning.

</details>


### [61] [SWE Atlas: Benchmarking Coding Agents Beyond Issue Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08366)
*Mohit Raghavendra,Soham Dan,Miguel Romero Calvo,Yannis Yiming He,Johannes Baptist Mols,Gautam Anand,Cole McCollum,Edgar Arakelyan,Vijay Bharadwaj,Andrew Park,Jeff Da,MohammadHossein Rezaei,Bing Liu,Brad Kenstler,Yunzhong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SWE Atlas 是一个针对编码智能体的基准测试套件，涵盖代码库问答（124项）、测试编写（90项）和重构（70项）三类专业软件工程工作流。它通过欠指定的智能体任务形式、类别特定的评估协议以及结合程序化检查与基于量规的评估框架，评估功能正确性和软件工程质量（如测试完整性、可维护性、可重用抽象、代码库卫生）。实验表明GPT-5.4和Opus 4.7表现最强，开源模型得分较低，顶级模型依赖广泛的代码库探索和运行时驱动的推理，但在边界情况、复杂运行时分析和工程最佳实践方面仍有困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SWE基准测试忽视了实际工作中重要但未被充分代表的类别（如测试编写、重构），且评估方式局限于功能正确性，缺乏对软件工程质量的全面衡量。SWE Atlas旨在填补这一空白，提供更贴近真实世界使用的评估。

Method: 构建了涵盖Codebase Q&A、Test Writing和Refactoring三类任务的基准套件（共284项任务），采用欠指定的智能体任务形式，设计类别特定的评估协议，结合程序化检查与基于量规的评估框架来衡量功能正确性和软件工程质量。

Result: GPT-5.4和Opus 4.7在SWE Atlas上表现最强，开源模型得分较低。顶级模型依赖广泛的代码库探索和运行时驱动的推理，但在处理细微边界情况、复杂运行时分析及遵循工程最佳实践方面仍存在一致性的困难。

Conclusion: SWE Atlas提供了一个互补的评估套件，能够同时衡量编码智能体的正确性和工程质量，揭示了当前模型在软件工程实践方面的不足。

Abstract: We introduce SWE Atlas, a benchmark suite for coding agents spanning three professional software engineering workflows: Codebase Q&A (124 tasks), Test Writing (90 tasks), and Refactoring (70 tasks). SWE Atlas differs from prior SWE benchmarks in three key ways: it targets underrepresented but practically important task categories, uses comprehensive category-specific evaluation protocols, and adopts under-specified, agentic task formulations that better reflect real-world usage. Its evaluation framework combines programmatic checks with rubric-based assessment. This goes beyond functional correctness, evaluating software engineering quality, including test and refactor completeness, maintainability, reusable abstractions, and codebase hygiene. We evaluate a range of frontier and open-weight models on SWE Atlas and find that GPT-5.4 and Opus 4.7 achieve the strongest overall performance, while even the best open-weight models score poorly. Our analysis suggests that top models rely on extensive codebase exploration and runtime-driven reasoning. However, even top models consistently struggle with subtle edge cases, complex runtime analysis, and adherence to software engineering best practices. Overall, SWE Atlas provides a complementary evaluation suite for measuring both correctness and engineering quality in coding agents.

</details>


### [62] [Reinforcement Learning for Scalable and Trustworthy Intelligent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08378)
*Guangchen Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文针对强化学习在实际部署中的两大挑战——分布式环境下的可扩展性（通信效率与异构计算）以及策略与人类偏好对齐及安全性（隐私保护）——通过四项贡献展开研究，涵盖联邦优化、偏好对齐和上下文安全三个方向，旨在同时提升强化学习的可扩展性和可信赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习虽已成为提升智能系统能力的强大范式，但其实际部署面临两大核心挑战：在分布式环境中，通信带宽有限且各智能体计算能力异构，强化学习需高效扩展；同时，强化学习越来越多地用于大语言模型后训练和自主智能体，优化后的策略必须与人类偏好对齐并满足安全性要求（如隐私感知的信息披露）。

Method: 本论文通过四项互补贡献展开研究：第一部分研究联邦设置下的可扩展强化学习；第二部分研究面向大语言模型的可信强化学习。具体方法包括通信高效和异步的联邦优化、偏好对齐以及上下文安全技术。

Result: 一方面，通过通信高效和异步的联邦优化提升了强化学习的可扩展性；另一方面，通过改进与人类偏好的对齐以及减少语言型智能系统中上下文不当的信息披露，增强了强化学习的可信赖性。

Conclusion: 下一代智能系统既需要高效的优化，也需要可信赖的行为，而强化学习为实现这两个目标提供了统一的框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become a powerful paradigm for improving the capability of intelligent systems, but its practical deployment faces two central challenges. First, reinforcement learning must scale efficiently in distributed environments where communication bandwidth is limited and computation is heterogeneous across agents. Second, as reinforcement learning is increasingly used in post-training large language models and autonomous agents, the optimized policies must also be aligned with human preferences and satisfy safety requirements such as privacy-aware information disclosure. This dissertation addresses both challenges through four complementary contributions spanning federated optimization, preference alignment, and contextual safety.
  The first part of the dissertation studies scalable reinforcement learning in federated settings. The second part of the dissertation studies trustworthy reinforcement learning for large language models. Together, these contributions advance reinforcement learning along two complementary dimensions. On the one hand, they make reinforcement learning more scalable through communication-efficient and asynchronous federated optimization. On the other hand, they make reinforcement learning more trustworthy by improving alignment with human preferences and by reducing contextually inappropriate information disclosure in language-based intelligent systems. As a whole, this dissertation argues that the next generation of intelligent systems will require both efficient optimization and trustworthy behavior, and that reinforcement learning provides a unifying framework for addressing both goals.

</details>


### [63] [The Power of Second Order Methods for Sequence Preconditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08390)
*Annie Marsden,Elad Hazan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将Vovk-Azoury-Warmuth (VAW)算法与通用序列预处理(USP)方法相结合，对于任意边际稳定的线性动力系统，实现了$O(\log^3 T)$的多对数遗憾界，解决了USP方法因预条件序列直径和梯度指数增长而无法达到最优遗憾界的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的序列预测方法在处理具有长记忆的动力系统（即边际稳定系统）时，遗憾值通常随生成模型的隐藏维度多项式增长。USP方法虽能将记忆需求从指数级压缩，但其预条件序列的直径和梯度呈指数爆炸，阻碍了最优遗憾界的实现。

Method: 基于最小描述长度原理，将VAW算法与USP结合：VAW天然适配USP框架，既能利用USP的记忆压缩优势，又对直径指数爆炸具有鲁棒性；同时通过提供新的复解析Chebyshev多项式界，将USP的适用性扩展到有界谱系统之外，允许处理具有恒定复数参数的系统。

Result: 对于任意边际稳定的线性动力系统（包括非对称隐藏转移矩阵），该算法实现了$O(\log^3 T)$的多对数遗憾界。

Conclusion: VAW算法与USP的结合可以突破USP在遗憾界上的瓶颈，并在更广的系统类别上取得优异的理论保证。

Abstract: Sequence prediction methods for dynamical systems with long memory, i.e. marginally stable systems, typically achieve regret that grows polynomially with the hidden dimension of the underlying generative model. Universal Sequence Preconditioning (USP) is a method that compresses any sequence which comes from a linear dynamical system into a "preconditioned" sequence which requires exponentially shorter memory for accurate prediction. However, the preconditioned sequence yields exponentially larger diameters and gradients, hindering USP from unlocking optimal regret bounds. Inspired by the minimum description length principle, we show that the Vovk-Azoury-Warmuth (VAW) algorithm is naturally matched to the USP regime. Indeed, it takes advantage of the memory compression while remaining robust to the exponential explosion of the diameter. We prove that combining USP with VAW achieves astoundingly strong results: for any marginally-stable linear dynamical system, this algorithm achieves polylogarithmic regret $O \left( \log^3 T \right)$ even in the presence of asymmetric hidden transition matrices. Finally, we extend the applicability of USP beyond bounded-spectrum systems by providing new complex-analytic bounds on Chebyshev polynomials, allowing for systems with constant complex arguments.

</details>


### [64] [Geometry-Aware Discretization Error of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08392)
*Samuel Hurault,Thomas Moreau,Gabriel Peyré*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对扩散采样中的离散化误差问题，在精确分数设置下推导了Euler-Maruyama方法的弱误差和Fréchet误差的一阶渐近展开式，特别在高斯数据下得到显式公式，揭示了离散化误差如何适应数据几何特征（协方差谱）以及与扩散调度、扩散项系数等参数的交互作用，并基于此提出几何感知的参数优化目标。实验表明，高斯公式的定性预测在不同几何特征的扩散采样问题（包括图像生成和后验采样）中保持鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非渐近分析虽然提供了收敛保证，但过于宽松且对扩散参数不敏感，无法指导实际设计——不同调度族得到相同的收敛速率，且依赖于粗糙的坏情况量（如维度或漂移Lipschitz常数）。作者希望采取一条不那么宏大但更具信息量的路径，推导离散化误差的渐近展开式，以揭示其与数据几何和扩散参数的精细依赖关系。

Method: 在精确分数假设下，推导Euler-Maruyama离散化的弱误差和Fréchet误差的一阶渐近展开式；针对一般光滑反向扩散给出公式，并在高斯数据下得到完全显式表达式；利用这些公式分析离散化误差如何通过协方差谱适应数据几何，以及如何与扩散调度和扩散项系数相互作用；提出基于这些几何感知量的参数优化目标；通过不同数据集上的图像生成和后验采样实验验证高斯公式定性预测的鲁棒性。

Result: 获得了Euler-Maruyama弱误差和Fréchet误差的一阶渐近展开式，在高斯数据下得到显式公式，揭示了离散化误差与协方差谱、扩散调度、扩散项系数的依赖关系；提出了可处理的几何感知参数优化目标；实验表明，高斯公式的定性预测在具有不同几何特征的扩散采样问题（包括图像生成和后验采样）中保持有效。

Conclusion: 通过渐近分析而非非渐近边界，可以更精细地刻画扩散采样中离散化误差的行为，揭示其与数据几何和扩散参数的依赖关系，为实际中的参数优化提供理论基础。

Abstract: Practical diffusion sampling is a numerical approximation problem: under a fixed inference budget, one must simulate a reverse-time ODE or SDE using only a limited number of denoising steps, so discretization error is often the dominant source of error. Existing non-asymptotic analyses provide convergence guarantees, but are typically too loose and too insensitive to diffusion parameters to guide practical design: broad families of schedules receive the same rates, which depend on coarse worst-case quantities such as the dimension or the drift Lipschitz constant. We take a less ambitious but more informative route. In the exact-score setting, we derive first-order asymptotic expansions of the Euler-Maruyama weak and Fréchet discretization errors. These formulas hold for general smooth reverse diffusions and become fully explicit under Gaussian data. They show how discretization error adapts to the geometry of the data through the covariance spectrum, and how this geometry interacts with key diffusion parameters, including the diffusion schedules and the diffusion-term coefficient. This yields tractable objectives for geometry-aware parameter optimization. Finally, we show that the qualitative predictions of the Gaussian formulas remain robust across diffusion sampling problems with different geometries, including image generation on different datasets and image posterior sampling.

</details>


### [65] [Exploring and Exploiting Stability in Latent Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08398)
*Rania Briq,Michael Kamp,Ohad Fried,Sarel Cohen,Stefan Kesselheim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明潜流匹配（LFM）模型对数据缩减和模型容量收缩等扰动具有鲁棒性，并利用这种稳定性实现了更高效的训练（减少数据需求）和推理（超过两倍的加速）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LFM模型在不同类型扰动下的稳定性表现，并利用该稳定性设计更高效的训练和推理算法。

Method: 1) 在显著缩减的数据集上训练LFM模型；2) 利用架构缩减下的稳定性，提出双模型粗到细方法：轻量模型负责FM轨迹第一阶段，高容量模型负责第二阶段；3) 引入三种样本评分标准筛选信息量高的样本。

Result: LFM在数据缩减和模型容量收缩下性能无显著下降；实现了数据节省和超过两倍的推理加速，同时生成质量可比。

Conclusion: LFM模型的稳定性是FM目标函数固有的特性，可被利用来大幅提升训练和推理效率。

Abstract: In this work, we show that Latent Flow-Matching (LFM) models are robust to different types of perturbations, including data reduction and model capacity shrinkage. We characterize this stability by their tendency to generate similar outputs under identical noise seeds. We provide a perspective relating this phenomenon to flow matching theory, which indicates that this stability is inherent to the FM objective. We further exploit this stability to derive practical algorithms for more efficient training and inference. Concretely, first, we show that by training LFM models on significantly reduced datasets, the performance does not degrade perceptually or quantitatively. This yields multiple advantages, such as reducing training time by converging faster under limited compute budget, and alleviating annotation effort when training conditional models. Second, LFM stability under architectural shrinkage gives rise to a two-model coarse-to-fine approach, one using a light-weight architecture for the first phase of the FM trajectory, and one with higher capacity for the second, thereby reducing the inference cost substantially. To determine which samples are informative, we introduce three sample-scoring criteria and evaluate them under standard metrics for generative models. Our results are thoroughly evaluated on multiple datasets, demonstrating the practical advantage of this stability, including data saving and a more than two-fold inference speedup while generating comparable outputs.

</details>


### [66] [Central Limit Theorem for Two-Time-Scale Approximate Distributionally Robust RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08417)
*Shengbo Wang,Zexi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对分布鲁棒强化学习（DRRL）在小模糊度KL散度集下的难题，提出一种基于鲁棒泛函一阶展开的近似框架，并设计出仅需单样本更新的模型无关算法MVSA，证明了其收敛性和中心极限定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布鲁棒强化学习中的鲁棒Bellman算子对转移核非线性，导致单样本更新有偏，且对抗优化使鲁棒评估计算复杂。现有方法难以兼顾模型无关性与计算效率。

Method: 利用KL散度模糊集下小模糊度区域的鲁棒泛函一阶泰勒展开，近似鲁棒Bellman方程以消除对抗优化；提出均方-方差随机逼近（MVSA）算法，通过提升的随机逼近动力学和双时间尺度设计实现单样本更新。

Result: 证明了MVSA的收敛性；建立了中心极限定理，其主迭代在$n^{-1/2}$标准速率下具有显式表征的渐近协方差；数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 本文提出的近似框架和MVSA算法有效解决了DRRL中模型无关算法的偏差与计算瓶颈，在理论上保证了估计量的渐近正态性，为实际应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Designing model-free algorithms for distributionally robust reinforcement learning (DRRL) poses fundamental challenges. The robust Bellman operator is nonlinear in the transition kernel, which makes one-sample Bellman updates biased, while the adversarial optimization underlying robustness makes robust evaluation computationally demanding. To address these difficulties, we consider the natural small-ambiguity regime under Kullback--Leibler ambiguity sets and propose an approximate DRRL framework based on a first-order expansion of the relevant robust functional. This yields an approximate robust Bellman equation that removes the adversarial optimization while remaining first-order accurate in the ambiguity radius. To learn the fixed point of this approximate equation, we propose Mean-Variance Stochastic Approximation (MVSA), a model-free algorithm that uses only one-sample updates. This is achieved via a lifted stochastic approximation dynamics and a two-time-scale design. We then prove convergence and a central limit theorem for MVSA: its main iterate satisfies a central limit theorem at the canonical $n^{-1/2}$ scale, with explicitly characterized asymptotic covariances. Finally, we validate our theoretical findings with a numerical experiment.

</details>


### [67] [Generalized Wasserstein Flow Matching: Transport Plans, Everywhere, All at Once](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08424)
*Moritz Piening,Richard Duong,Gabriele Steidl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种Wasserstein-on-Wasserstein (WoW) 框架，将流匹配扩展到概率测度空间，通过嵌套Wasserstein几何实现元测度流，并利用切片和线性Wasserstein距离进行高效近似，统一了点云和集合生成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流匹配方法局限于单一概率测度空间，无法处理"测度上的测度"这类层次化结构（如点云分布），缺乏一个能够捕捉元分布间传输动态的理论框架。

Method: 将流匹配扩展到Wasserstein-on-Wasserstein空间，利用嵌套Wasserstein几何构造内层和外层传输计划；为降低计算成本，引入切片Wasserstein距离和线性Wasserstein距离进行可扩展近似，保证近直线轨迹和数值稳定性。

Result: 提出了一种统一且理论完备的元测度流生成框架，通过近似方法实现了高效训练，在点云和集合生成任务上统一并扩展了现有方法。

Conclusion: WoW流匹配为层次化概率分布的生成建模提供了有理论依据且实用的方法，通过可扩展近似平衡了计算效率与几何保真度。

Abstract: Flow matching has recently emerged as a flexible and efficient framework for generative modelling by learning deterministic transport dynamics between probability measures. In this work, we extend flow matching to the space of probability measures over probability measures, introducing a Wasserstein-on-Wasserstein (WoW) formulation. Leveraging the nested Wasserstein geometry, we show that measures over transport plans naturally induce velocity fields that realize metameasure flows. This yields a principled generalization of Wasserstein flow matching via coupled outer and inner transport plans. To address the substantial computational cost of WoW transport, we propose scalable approximations based on sliced and linear Wasserstein distances, enabling efficient training while promoting numerically stable, near-straight trajectories. Our framework unifies and extends existing approaches to point cloud and set generation, providing a practical and theoretically grounded method for generative modelling in WoW spaces.

</details>


### [68] [TARO: Temporal Adversarial Rectification Optimization Using Diffusion Models as Purifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08440)
*Daniel Wesego,Pedram Rooshenas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TARO方法，利用扩散轨迹中多个去噪视图构建时间引导的得分先验，通过粗到细的残差目标实现对抗净化的鲁棒性和语义保持平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散净化方法在自适应攻击下容易失效，而测试时得分优化虽更鲁棒但通常使用单一噪声 regime 或均匀处理时间步，忽略了粗粒度和细粒度去噪尺度的不同作用。

Method: TARO从扩散轨迹中多个去噪视图构建时间引导的得分先验，形成粗到细的残差目标：高噪声专家提供全局平滑结构降低对抗敏感性，低噪声专家恢复图像特异性类别相关细节，并通过引导强度控制时间校正来平衡鲁棒全局修正和语义保持。

Result: 在多个数据集和自适应威胁模型下，TARO以零样本方式提升了鲁棒准确率，并可与对抗似然目标兼容以获得进一步鲁棒性提升。

Conclusion: 通过时间引导的多尺度去噪先验，TARO有效平衡了鲁棒性和语义保持，是对现有扩散对抗净化方法的重要改进。

Abstract: Adversarial purification with diffusion models seeks to project adversarial examples back toward the data manifold, but balancing semantic preservation and robustness against adaptive attacks remains challenging. Recent work shows that standard diffusion purification can fail under adaptive evaluation, while test-time score-based optimization is more resilient. Existing optimization defenses, however, typically rely on a single diffusion noise regime or treat timesteps uniformly, overlooking the distinct roles of coarse and fine denoising scales. We propose Temporal Adversarial Rectification Optimization (TARO), an inference-time purification method that builds a temporally guided score prior from multiple denoising views along the diffusion trajectory. TARO forms a coarse-to-fine residual target: high-noise experts provide globally smoothed structure with reduced adversarial sensitivity, while low-noise experts restore image-specific, class-relevant details. A guidance strength controls this temporal correction, allowing TARO to balance robust global rectification with semantic preservation. Empirically, TARO improves robust accuracy across datasets and adaptive threat models in a zero-shot setting, while remaining compatible with complementary adversarial-likelihood objectives for further robustness gains.

</details>


### [69] [Zero-shot Imitation Learning by Latent Topology Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08450)
*Maxwell J. Jacobson,Yexiang Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZALT是一种模仿学习方法，通过识别轨迹中的潜在枢纽状态，构建枢纽间转移的拓扑结构，并在此拓扑上进行规划，从而实现对未见过的起始-目标任务的零样本适应。在复杂3D迷宫环境中，ZALT在未见任务上达到55%零样本成功率，而最强基线仅为6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长期目标条件任务中，固定演示数据集虽然包含有用行为，但缺少每个任务的完整示例。现有模仿学习方法在长时域任务中会累积小误差，导致对新任务的零样本适应不可靠。

Method: ZALT首先识别轨迹中轨迹汇聚或分叉的潜在枢纽状态，然后学习枢纽到枢纽转移的策略和动力学模型，最后在枢纽拓扑上进行规划以完成新任务。该方法使演示行为显式可组合，同时将长任务压缩为更短的抽象转移序列。

Result: 在复杂3D迷宫环境中，ZALT在未见过的起始-目标任务上达到55%零样本成功率，而最强基线仅为6%。

Conclusion: ZALT通过构建潜在枢纽拓扑结构，实现了演示行为的显式组合与长任务压缩，在长期时域、目标条件任务中展现出强大的零样本适应能力，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Imitation learning is effective for training agents when expert demonstrations are available, but collecting demonstrations for every complex task in an environment is costly. We study the long-horizon, goal-conditioned setting where a fixed demonstration dataset contains useful behavior, but not complete examples for every task the agent must solve. Existing imitation learning methods can learn strong policies from demonstrations, but when solving long-horizon tasks, small errors accumulate over long primitive-action trajectories and make zero-shot adaptation to new tasks unreliable. We introduce Zero-shot Agents from Latent Topologies (ZALT), an imitation-learning method that solves unseen start-goal tasks beyond those demonstrated during training. ZALT identifies latent hub states where trajectories converge or diverge, learns policies and a dynamics model over hub-to-hub transitions, and plans over the hub topology to complete new tasks. This topology makes demonstrated behaviors explicitly composable while compressing long tasks into shorter sequences of abstract transitions -- combined, these enable ZALT to perform zero-shot adaptation. In a complex 3D maze environment, ZALT achieves 55% zero-shot success on unseen tasks, compared to 6% for the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [70] [RubiConv -- Efficient Boundary-Respecting Convolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08451)
*Linda Friso,Annie Marsden,Xinyi Chen,Arushi Gupta,Peter Bartlett,Mark Braverman,Elad Hazan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RubiConv算法，解决卷积架构在打包序列上使用FFT时存在的边界问题，使得长卷积模型的理论效率在大规模数据打包场景下得以实际落地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卷积架构虽在序列建模中具有理论复杂度优势（通过FFT），但标准FFT无法兼容数据打包（document packing）这一大规模训练中的关键硬件优化手段，导致实际性能远低于理论预期。

Method: 提出RubiConv算法，一种支持硬件高效、尊重边界（boundary-respecting）的卷积计算方法，专门针对打包序列设计。

Result: 实验表明RubiConv在速度和效率上显著超越注意力机制和标准FFT基线方法。

Conclusion: RubiConv将长卷积模型的理论效率转化为大规模真实数据打包场景下的实际可用性。

Abstract: Convolutional architectures have emerged as powerful alternatives to Transformers for sequence modeling. The primary advantage is that they offer improved theoretical sequence length complexity by leveraging the Fast Fourier Transform (FFT). However, this theoretical improvement does not always meaningfully land in practice. One critical obstacle is that applying standard FFTs is not amenable to the large-scale training pipeline wherein data is packed from different sources into a single sequence for hardware efficiency. Indeed, standard FFT algorithms are not easily amenable to document packing. Existing workarounds suffer from severe inefficiencies, crippling the practical performance of convolutional architectures. We close this gap with RubiConv, a novel algorithm for performing hardware-efficient, boundary-respecting convolutions on packed sequences. Extensive experiments show that RubiConv achieves significant speedups over both attention and standard FFT-based baselines. This work makes the theoretical efficiency of long convolutional models a practical reality for large-scale, real-world data packing.

</details>


### [71] [Sink vs. diagonal patterns as mechanisms for attention switch and oversmoothing prevention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08453)
*Peter Súkeník,Cristina López Amado,Christoph H. Lampert,Marco Mondelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了注意力机制中的sinks（注意力陷阱）和对角线模式在注意力切换及抗过平滑中的作用，分析了sinks的几何表示条件、密集与稀疏注意力对平滑的影响差异、sinks与硬注意力切换的等价性，并通过引入对角线模式解释了预训练变压器为何偏好sinks而非对角线模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对sinks在抗过平滑中的作用理解尚不完善，且缺乏对对角线模式（即token仅与自身通信）的系统分析。本文旨在弥合过平滑预防所需条件与sinks实际提供机制之间的差距，并解释注意力层在不需要token通信时为何表现得像MLP。

Method: 通过几何分析推导sink嵌入与其他所有嵌入之间的必要对齐条件；理论上证明密集注意力比稀疏注意力更易导致平滑的条件，并进行实证验证；证明sinks与硬注意力开关（输出恒为0）之间的等价性；通过量化比较sinks与对角线模式的表示成本，解释预训练模型对sinks的偏好。

Result: 1) 揭示了sink嵌入必须与其他所有嵌入对齐的几何条件；2) 明确了密集注意力在特定条件下比稀疏注意力更易导致过平滑，且这些条件在实际中常被满足；3) 建立了sinks与硬注意力开关的等价关系；4) 量化表明sinks的表示成本低于对角线模式，解释了预训练模型为何偏好sinks。

Conclusion: sinks和对角线模式作为注意力开关和抗过平滑机制具有重要作用，sinks因其更低的表示成本在预训练变压器中被偏好。对角线模式的引入和对注意力开关的推广填补了过平滑预防需求与sinks实际能力之间的理论空白，同时揭示了注意力层在不需要token间通信时如何退化为MLP行为。

Abstract: This paper studies the role of sinks and diagonal patterns as attention switch and anti-oversmoothing mechanisms. We analyze geometric conditions under which sinks can be represented, showing a necessary alignment between the embedding of the sink and all other embeddings. Next, we refine the current understanding of the role of sinks in oversmoothing prevention: we specify the conditions under which dense attention provably smooths more than sparse attention, and empirically verify that such conditions are often satisfied in practice. We further prove an equivalence between sinks and hard attention switch, in which the output of the attention is identically 0. Finally, we relax the hard attention switch by allowing token self-communication: we provide a quantitative comparison of the costs of representing sinks vs.\ diagonal patterns, showing why sinks are favored in pretrained transformers. The introduction and analysis of diagonal patterns and the generalization of the attention switch close the gap between what oversmoothing prevention requires and what sinks provide, while also establishing when and why attention layers act like MLPs if token communication is not necessary.

</details>


### [72] [Recovering Physical Dynamics from Discrete Observations via Intrinsic Differential Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08454)
*Yuxiang Luo,Andrew Perrault*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用自治动力系统的半群性质作为全局结构约束来训练时间条件割线速度场，通过对称性破缺度量同时作为训练正则化和自适应求解器的推理依据，在扩散-反应基准上实现了87%的RMSE降低并减少5倍函数评估量，在自回归设定下以更少函数评估保持最低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从离散观测恢复连续时间动力学时，局部监督（点回归、导数近似、方程残差）在观测间隔较大时保真度下降，现有方法缺乏跨时间尺度的全局一致性约束。

Method: 提出对称性破缺（Symmetry Rupture）框架：1) 训练时间条件割线速度场，以半群性质的偏差作为正则化项；2) 推理时利用该偏差替代局部截断误差，自适应选择最大步长以保持内部一致性。

Result: 扩散-反应基准上，时间信息推理设定下RMSE降低87%，函数评估减少5倍；三个PDE基准中的两个在直接自回归设定下取得最低RMSE，基线方法发散或需多一个数量级的函数评估才能保持稳定。

Conclusion: 全局半群约束比局部监督更具泛化性，对称性破缺可作为自适应求解器的高效替代准则，在计算效率与精度间取得更好权衡。

Abstract: Recovering continuous-time dynamics from discrete observations is difficult because local supervision (e.g., pointwise regression targets, derivative approximations, or equation residuals) loses fidelity as the observation interval grows. We replace local supervision with a global structural constraint: any flow representing autonomous dynamics must satisfy the semi-group property under time translation. We train a time-conditioned secant velocity field whose deviation from this property, which we call Symmetry Rupture, serves two purposes. As a training regularizer, it confines the hypothesis space to flows that compose consistently across temporal scales. As an inference oracle, it lets the solver select the largest step size that preserves internal consistency, replacing the local truncation error that conventional adaptive solvers depend on. On the diffusion-reaction benchmark under time-informed inference, our method reduces rollout RMSE by 87\% while using 5x fewer function evaluations than a Neural ODE baseline. In the more demanding direct auto-regressive setting, where the model must predict distant future frames without intermediate temporal cues, our adaptive solver allocates compute based on local geometric complexity -- maintaining the lowest rollout RMSE on two of three PDE benchmarks while baselines either diverge or require up to an order of magnitude more function evaluations to remain stable.

</details>


### [73] [CUDABeaver: Benchmarking LLM-Based Automated CUDA Debugging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08455)
*Shiyang Li,Haoyang Chen,Mattia Fazzini,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CUDABEAVER是一个用于评估LLM修复CUDA程序能力的基准测试，包含213个真实失败案例。它通过协议条件化指标pass@k(M,C,A)评估修复效果，发现宽松性能容差下修复器表现良好，但严格性能要求可使成功率下降40个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CUDA调试因硬件行为、编译器决策、内存层次和异步执行间的微妙交互而极具挑战性。现有LLM编程评估忽略了"退化性修复"问题——模型通过简化代码使测试通过，但牺牲了原始优化结构。需要更真实的基准来评估LLM修复CUDA缺陷的能力。

Method: 构建CUDABEAVER基准，包含从LLM生成CUDA过程中产生的真实失败工作空间。每个任务提供损坏的候选代码、原生构建/测试命令、原始错误证据和单个可编辑文件。提出pass@k(M,C,A)协议条件化指标，将修复器M、语料库C和协议轴A明确分离，从失败类别、调试轨迹、停滞模式和性能保持四个维度报告结果。

Result: 在213个任务和7个前沿LLM上的实验表明，协议感知评估能更忠实地反映CUDA调试能力。当性能损失容忍度高时，修复器表现强劲；但即使微小的严格性能要求也会显著降低测量成功率，评分波动可达40个百分点。

Conclusion: 现有LLM修复CUDA的评估存在严重泡沫，忽视性能保持要求会高估模型能力。CUDABEAVER和pass@k(M,C,A)指标提供了更真实、更严格的评估框架，揭示了LLM在CUDA调试中的真实局限性。

Abstract: Debugging CUDA programs has long been challenging because failures often arise from subtle interactions among hardware behavior, compiler decisions, memory hierarchy, and asynchronous execution. More importantly, with the rapid expansion of GPU usage across scientific computing, machine learning, graphics, and systems workloads, CUDA debugging has become more challenging than ever. Current evaluations of LLM-based CUDA programming largely miss this setting: a model can pass correctness tests with repair by degeneration, simplifying the CUDA code into a safer but slower program that abandons the original optimization structure. We introduce CUDABEAVER, a benchmark for CUDA debugging from real failing workspaces produced during LLM-based CUDA generation.
