Each task provides the broken candidate, native build/test commands, raw error evidence, and a single editable file. CUDABEAVER evaluates whether a fixer truly repairs the failing CUDA code or merely finds a slower test-passing replacement, reporting results by failure category, debugging trajectory, stagnation mode, and performance preservation. We further propose pass@k(M,C,A), a protocol-conditional CUDA debugging metric by making the fixer M, corpus C, and protocol axes Aexplicit. Using this metric across 213 tasks and seven frontier LLMs, we show that protocol-aware evaluation gives a more faithful view of CUDA debugging ability: when performance-loss tolerance is high, fixers appear much stronger, but even a minor stricter performance requirement can sharply reduce measured success, shifting scores by up to 40 percentage points.

</details>


### [74] [Neurally-plausible radial basis kernels using distributed Fourier embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08458)
*Jakeb Chouinard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在神经可解释的空间语义指针框架中可实现的径向基核函数，分析基于网格细胞表征的径向基核工作，证明此类表征既能实现径向基核函数且具有最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连贯、连续的空间表征对于将物理和感知现象综合到单一表征空间中至关重要，径向基核函数为这种分布式表征提供了前进方向。

Method: 在神经可解释的空间语义指针框架中表征并分析常见的径向基核函数，并对基于网格细胞表征的径向基核工作进行理论分析。

Result: 证明了基于网格细胞样式的表征既能实现径向基核函数，且在该任务上具有最优性。

Conclusion: 基于网格细胞的表征在实现径向基核函数方面具有能力优势和最优性。

Abstract: Coherent, continuous spatial representations are critical for synthesizing physical and perceptual phenomena into a single representational space. Radial basis kernels provide a path forward for this type of distributed representation. In this work, we aim to characterize and analyze common radial basis kernels realizable in the neurally-plausible framework of spatial semantic pointers. Further, we analyze previous radial basis kernel work based on grid cell-like representations and demonstrate that such representations are both capable of and optimal for realizing radial basis kernels.

</details>


### [75] [CUDAHercules: Benchmarking Hardware-Aware Expert-level CUDA Optimization for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08467)
*Shiyang Li,Zijian Zhang,Guangyan Sun,Yuebo Luo,Winson Chen,Yanzhi Wang,Mingyi Hong,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 大型语言模型在自动化CUDA编程方面有潜力，但即使最先进的编码模型（如Claude-Opus-4.6）也远未达到专家级架构感知优化水平。我们提出了CUDAHercules基准测试，涵盖单内核、模块级算子、完整应用及未解决挑战任务，覆盖Ampere、Hopper和Blackwell GPU架构。评估显示，模型生成的CUDA代码虽可编译通过测试，但难以恢复专家级优化策略，且应用层语义进一步降低了成功率。迭代或工具增强反馈虽能提升正确性，却会导致性能退化至缓慢的备选实现。结果表明，自动化CUDA编程远未解决，需要更强的硬件推理能力、更好的工具使用以及将代码理解与硬件架构智能相结合的训练目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在CUDA代码生成方面虽能生成可运行代码，但与专家级架构感知优化之间存在巨大鸿沟，缺乏系统性的基准测试来量化这一差距并揭示模型在硬件底层优化策略上的不足。

Method: 提出CUDAHercules基准测试，涵盖单内核、模块级算子、完整应用及未解决挑战任务四种粒度，覆盖Ampere、Hopper、Blackwell三代GPU架构，并采用领域特定的语义验证器对端到端任务进行门控评估。在Claude-Opus-4.6和GPT-5.4等模型上进行系统测评，同时引入迭代反馈和工具增强机制进行分析。

Result: 模型生成的CUDA代码虽能编译并通过功能测试，但很少能恢复专家级优化策略以匹配SOTA性能；应用层语义进一步降低了成功率；迭代或工具增强反馈虽能改善正确性，但会导致模型向缓慢的备选实现漂移，造成性能退化。

Conclusion: 自动化CUDA编程仍未解决，现存模型的硬件架构推理能力不足，需要更强的硬件推理、更好的工具使用以及将代码理解与硬件架构智能相结合的训练目标来弥合这一差距。

Abstract: Large language models show promise for automated CUDA programming, however even the strongest coding models (e.g., Claude-Opus-4.6) may still fall short of expert-level, architecture-aware optimization. We introduce CUDAHercules, a benchmark that evaluates generated CUDA against end-to-end human-expert SOTA systems. It spans single kernels, module-level operators, full applications, and unsolved challenge tasks across Ampere, Hopper, and Blackwell GPUs, with end-to-end tasks gated by domain-specific semantic validators. Evaluating models such as Claude-Opus-4.6 and GPT-5.4 shows a large gap between runnable CUDA and expert CUDA engineering: models often compile and pass tests, but rarely recover the optimization strategies needed to match expert performance. Application semantics further reduce success, and iterative or tool-augmented feedback can improve correctness while drifting toward slow fallback implementations. These results show that automated CUDA programming remains far from fully solved and requires stronger hardware reasoning, better tool use, and training objectives that connect code understanding to hardware architecture-grounded intelligence.

</details>


### [76] [Transformers Can Implement Preconditioned Richardson Iteration for In-Context Gaussian Kernel Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08475)
*Mingsong Yan,Dongyang Li,Charles Kulick,Sui Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明标准softmax注意力Transformer可在前向传播中通过实现预处理Richardson迭代来近似高斯核的上下文核岭回归（KRR）预测器，并提供了理论构造和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于上下文学习（ICL）的机制解释主要针对线性回归任务，对于非线性ICL，虽然已有工作将softmax和核化注意力与函数梯度型动力学联系起来，但尚不清楚标准softmax注意力Transformer能否实现具有端到端预测误差保证的收敛求解器。

Method: 理论方面，作者构造了一个O(log(1/ε))块和MLP宽度O(√(N/ε))的单头Transformer，证明其可实现ε-精度预测；架构分解为softmax注意力生成行归一化高斯核算子，ReLU MLP层近似标量算术更新。实验方面，在GP回归任务上训练GPT-2风格Transformer，通过线性探针比较逐层预测与经典KRR求解器的步进输出。

Result: 理论构造在有限数据假设下达到ε-精度预测；实验发现Transformer的误差剖面与预处理Richardson迭代最一致，消融研究进一步支持该解释。

Conclusion: 预处理Richardson迭代是softmax注意力Transformer在非线性上下文高斯核回归中可以实现的具体机制。

Abstract: Mechanistic accounts of in-context learning (ICL) have identified iterative algorithms for linear regression and related linear prediction tasks, often using linear or ReLU attention variants. For nonlinear ICL, prior work has related softmax and kernelized attention to functional-gradient-type dynamics, but it remains unclear whether a standard transformer with softmax attention can implement a convergent solver with an end-to-end prediction-error guarantee. In this paper, we study in-context kernel ridge regression (KRR) with Gaussian kernels and show that a standard softmax-attention transformer can approximate the KRR predictor during its forward pass by implementing preconditioned Richardson iteration on the associated kernel linear system. Under bounded-data assumptions, we construct a single-head transformer with $O(\log(1/ε))$ blocks and MLP width $O(\sqrt{N/ε})$ that achieves $ε$-accurate prediction for prompts of length $N$. Our construction reveals a functional decomposition within the transformer architecture: softmax attention produces a row-normalized Gaussian-kernel operator needed for cross-token interactions, while ReLU MLP layers act locally to approximate the intra-token scalar arithmetic required by the update. Empirically, we train GPT-2-style transformers on Gaussian-process regression tasks to further test the preconditioned Richardson interpretation. Through linear probing, we compare the transformer's layer-wise predictions with the step-wise outputs of classical KRR solvers and find that its error profiles align most consistently with preconditioned Richardson iteration. Ablation studies further support this interpretation. Together, our theory and experiments identify preconditioned Richardson iteration as a concrete mechanism that softmax-attention transformers can realize for nonlinear in-context Gaussian-kernel regression.

</details>


### [77] [ShifaMind: A Multiplicative Concept Bottleneck for Interpretable ICD-10 Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08482)
*Mohammed Sameer Syed,Xuan Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ShifaMind架构，通过乘法概念瓶颈（MCB）改变瓶颈形式而非宽度，在保持概念可解释性的同时提升预测性能，在MIMIC-IV top-50 ICD-10编码任务上达到与最强基线LAAT竞争的性能，并显著优于传统概念瓶颈模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统概念瓶颈模型（CBM）在提供可解释性时，狭窄的概念层会限制梯度流动和预测能力，因此需要设计一种既能保持概念级可解释性又不牺牲模型容量的瓶颈结构。

Method: ShifaMind采用乘法概念瓶颈（MCB），通过学习乘法门控机制作用于概念引导的表示，同时保留标量概念接口供检查，改变了瓶颈的形式而非宽度。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV top-50 ICD-10编码任务上，ShifaMind在F1、AUC和排序指标上与最强基线LAAT竞争，超越其他五个ICD编码基线，并在预测性能和可解释性指标上大幅优于同等容量的Vanilla CBM。

Conclusion: 瓶颈设计形式对概念瓶颈模型至关重要，ShifaMind证明了通过改变瓶颈形式而非宽度可以同时实现高预测性能和概念级可解释性。

Abstract: Automated ICD-10 coding from clinical discharge summaries requires models that are both accurate on long-tailed multi-label classification tasks and interpretable to clinicians. Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) offer a principled framework for interpretability by routing predictions through human-interpretable concepts, but this transparency often comes at a cost: compressing rich clinical text representations into a narrow concept layer can restrict gradient flow and limit predictive capacity. We present ShifaMind, a concept-grounded architecture built around a Multiplicative Concept Bottleneck (MCB), which changes the form, rather than the width, of the bottleneck. Instead of projecting through a narrow concept layer, ShifaMind uses a learned multiplicative gate over a concept-grounded representation while retaining a scalar concept interface for inspection. On MIMIC-IV top-50 ICD-10 coding, ShifaMind achieves performance competitive with LAAT, the strongest baseline, across F1, AUC, and ranking metrics, while outperforming five additional ICD-coding baselines and providing concept-mediated explanations. Its substantial gains over a capacity-matched Vanilla CBM in both predictive performance and interpretability-oriented metrics highlight the importance of the bottleneck design.

</details>


### [78] [NeuralBench: A Unifying Framework to Benchmark NeuroAI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08495)
*Hubert Banville,Stéphane d'Ascoli,Simon Dahan,Jérémy Rapin,Marlène Careil,Yohann Benchetrit,Jarod Lévy,Saarang Panchavati,Antoine Ratouchniak,Mingfang,Zhang,Elisa Cascardi,Katelyn Begany,Teon Brooks,Jean-Rémi King*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuralBench是一个统一的脑活动AI模型基准测试框架，配套的NeuralBench-EEG v1.0包含36个EEG任务、14种深度学习架构和94个数据集。初步发现：现有基础模型仅略优于任务专用模型；许多任务（如认知解码、临床预测）仍极具挑战性。框架支持扩展至MEG和fMRI等其他神经影像模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前脑记录AI模型缺乏系统评估：预处理流程、训练/微调方法差异大，下游评估局限于少量任务和数据集，亟需统一的基准测试标准。

Method: 提出了NeuralBench统一框架，并发布EEG v1.0版本，包含36个EEG任务、14种深度学习架构，在94个数据集上通过标准化接口进行评测。框架设计支持新任务、数据集、模型和模态的集成。

Result: 1) 当前基础模型仅略优于任务专用模型；2) 大量任务（认知解码、临床预测等）即使对最佳模型仍极具挑战性；3) 初步扩展到MEG和fMRI展示了框架的跨模态能力。

Conclusion: NeuralBench为神经影像AI模型提供了统一的基准测试标准，邀请社区共同扩展这一开源框架，推动标准化评估体系的建立。

Abstract: Deep learning and large public datasets have recently catalyzed the proliferation of AI models for processing brain recordings. However, systematically evaluating these models remains a challenge: not only do the preprocessing pipelines, training and finetuning approaches largely vary across studies, but their downstream evaluation is often limited to small sets of tasks and/or datasets. Here, we present NeuralBench: a unified framework for benchmarking AI models of brain activity. We accompany this framework with NeuralBench-EEG v1.0 -- a large EEG benchmark that includes 36 electroencephalography (EEG) tasks and 14 deep learning architectures, and is evaluated on 94 datasets accessed through a standardized interface. This first EEG-focused release already highlights two main findings. First, current foundation models only marginally outperform task-specific models. Second, a large set of tasks (e.g. cognitive decoding, clinical predictions) remain highly challenging, even for the best models. Critically, NeuralBench is designed for the integration of new tasks, datasets, models, and neuroimaging modalities, as illustrated by preliminary extensions to MEG and fMRI datasets and models. Through this white paper, we invite the community to expand this open-source framework and work together toward a unified benchmarking standard for neuroimaging models.

</details>


### [79] [Scaling Limits of Long-Context Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08505)
*Giuseppe Bruno,Shi Chen,Zhengjiang Lin,Yury Polyanskiy,Philippe Rigollet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了softmax自注意力在长上下文极限下的行为，以逆温度β_n为缩放参数，揭示了注意力从均匀平均到单键坍塌的临界尺度由距离-查询分布近零的局部指数决定（均匀球面上β_n^* ≍ n^{2/(d-1)}），并刻画了亚临界、临界和超临界三个 regime 下的注意力权重与输出的极限律，其中亚临界情形下注意力近似实现反向热方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解softmax自注意力在长上下文（大量键）下的行为极限，特别是注意力何时退化为均匀平均、何时坍缩到最近键，以及决定这一转变的关键尺度。

Method: 固定查询，随机上下文为球面上i.i.d.键，以逆温度β_n为缩放参数，通过分析距离-查询分布在零点附近的局部指数，推导临界尺度β_n^* ≍ n^{2/(d-1)}，并分别在亚临界、临界和超临界 regime 下推导注意力权重与输出的极限分布。

Result: 找到了注意力从均匀到选择性坍缩的临界尺度；刻画了三个 regime 下有序注意力权重和注意力输出的极限律：亚临界下输出为查询附近的局部平均，带有确定偏差和高斯波动；临界下最近邻键保留宏观质量但无单键坍缩；超临界下所有质量集中于最近键。亚临界且值为单位矩阵时，注意力近似实现反向热方程。

Conclusion: 长上下文下softmax注意力的行为完全由距离分布的局部几何决定，不同β_n标度导致截然不同的注意力聚合行为。

Abstract: We study the long-context limit of softmax self-attention with a fixed query and a random context of $n$ i.i.d. keys on the sphere, viewing the inverse temperature $β_n$ as the scaling parameter that decides whether attention degenerates into uniform averaging or collapses onto the single closest key. We show that the critical scale at which selectivity emerges is determined by the local exponent of the distance-to-query distribution near zero rather than by global features of the context, and scales like $β_n^\ast \asymp n^{2/(d-1)}$ for uniform keys on $\mathbb{S}^{d-1}$. Furthermore, we characterize the limiting laws of the ordered attention weights and of the attention output across all regimes of $β_n$: a subcritical regime in which the output reduces to a local average around $q$ with explicit deterministic bias and Gaussian fluctuations; a critical regime in which a finite collection of nearest keys retains macroscopic mass without single-key collapse; and a supercritical regime in which all mass concentrates on the closest key. Of notable interest is the subcritical case with identity value matrix where the attention map approximately implements a backward heat equation.

</details>


### [80] [Learning Polyhedral Conformal Sets for Robust Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08506)
*Shuyi Chen,Wenbin Zhou,Shixiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种决策感知的共形框架，通过学习参数化多面体不确定集来优化鲁棒优化目标，在保证统计有效性的同时降低决策保守性，并提供了有限样本覆盖保证和最优性界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒优化的性能严重依赖于不确定集的选择：过大的集合导致决策过于保守，过小的集合可能排除真实结果。现有数据驱动方法（如共形预测）虽能提供有限样本有效性保证，但忽视了下游决策结构，缺乏任务特异性。

Method: 1) 参数化灵活的多面体不确定集族，通过数据驱动超平面定义；2) 直接最小化诱导的鲁棒损失来学习集合几何；3) 通过共形校准保持统计有效性；4) 在独立数据集上执行重校准步骤以纠正数据依赖选择带来的偏差。

Result: 学习到的不确定集能够捕捉与决策目标对齐的方向性和各向异性不确定性，同时保持计算可处理性；提供了有限样本覆盖保证和与oracle决策之间的次优性界。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了统计有效性与决策最优性之间的差距，为数据驱动的鲁棒优化提供了一个有原则的框架。

Abstract: Robust optimization (RO) provides a principled framework for decision-making under uncertainty, but its performance critically depends on the choice of the uncertainty set. While large sets ensure reliability, they often lead to overly conservative decisions, whereas small sets risk excluding the true outcome. Recent data-driven approaches, particularly conformal prediction, offer finite-sample validity guarantees but remain largely task-agnostic, ignoring the downstream decision structure. In this paper, we propose a decision-aware conformal framework that learns uncertainty sets tailored to robust optimization objectives. Our approach parameterizes a flexible family of polyhedral sets via data-driven hyperplanes and learns their geometry by directly minimizing the induced robust loss, while preserving statistical validity through conformal calibration. To correct for data-dependent selection, we incorporate a re-calibration step on an independent dataset to restore coverage. The resulting sets capture directional and anisotropic uncertainty aligned with the decision objective while remaining computationally tractable. We provide finite-sample coverage guarantees and bounds on the sub-optimality gap to an oracle decision. This work bridges the gap between statistical validity and decision optimality, providing a principled framework for data-driven robust optimization.

</details>


### [81] [MoMo: Conditioned Contrastive Representation Learning for Preference-Modulated Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08512)
*Yusuf Syed,Viraj Parimi,Brian Williams*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoMo是一种偏好条件化的对比规划器，通过特征线性调制和低秩神经调制学习联合条件表征几何和潜在预测算子，在无需重新训练的情况下，在推理时通过标量用户偏好连续调节规划的保守程度，实现任务效率与风险暴露的灵活权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比规划工作学习单一潜在几何结构，无法区分在相同起始-目标查询下，多个在任务效率和风险暴露之间进行权衡的有效行为。

Method: MoMo通过特征线性调制（FiLM）调节表征几何，通过低秩神经调制调节潜在预测算子，实现偏好条件化的联合学习，并保持对比规划所需的概率密度比编码。

Result: 在六个环境中，MoMo能够根据用户偏好平滑地调整规划安全性，在时间一致性和偏好一致性上优于状态增强基线方法。

Conclusion: 该公式保留了推理驱动对比规划所需的表征空间中的概率密度比编码，同时保持了推理时的计算效率。

Abstract: Temporally contrastive representation learning induces a latent structure capable of reducing long-horizon planning to inference in a low-dimensional linear system. However, existing contrastive planning work learns a single latent geometry which cannot distinguish multiple valid behaviors trading task efficiency against risk exposure for the same start-goal query. We introduce MoMo, a preference-conditioned contrastive planner allowing a scalar user preference to continuously modulate plan conservativeness at inference time, without retraining. MoMo learns a joint conditioning of the representation geometry and latent prediction operator via Feature-Wise Linear Modulation and low-rank neural modulation, respectively. We show that our formulation preserves the probability density ratio encoded in the representation space that is required for inference-driven contrastive planning, further retaining its inference-time efficiency. Across six environments, MoMo smoothly adapts plan safety according to user preferences, yielding improved temporal and preferential consistency over state augmentation baselines.

</details>


### [82] [SeBA: Semi-supervised few-shot learning via Separated-at-Birth Alignment for tabular data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08519)
*Kacper Jurek,Wojciech Batko,Marek Śmieja,Marcin Przewięźlikowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SeBA，一种无需数据增强的联合嵌入框架，用于表格数据的半监督少样本学习，通过将数据分离为两个独立但互补的视图并对齐表示，在多数基准数据集上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半监督少样本学习方法依赖自监督学习框架，而自监督学习需要数据增强；对表格数据而言，定义有意义的数据增强非常困难且容易扭曲语义，限制了传统自监督学习的有效性。

Method: 提出Separated-at-Birth Alignment (SeBA)，一种联合嵌入框架：将数据分离为两个独立但互补的视图，将第一个视图的表示对齐到第二个视图中数据的最近邻对应关系，从而消除对数据增强的依赖。

Result: 理论分析证明SeBA能生成改善特征-标签关系的输出空间。在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，SeBA在大多数情况下达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: SeBA开辟了表格数据领域半监督少样本学习的新范式，通过消除数据增强依赖，有效解决了表格数据自监督学习的核心难题。

Abstract: Learning from scarce labeled data with a larger pool of unlabeled samples, known as semi-supervised few-shot learning (SS-FSL), remains critical for applications involving tabular data in domains like medicine, finance, and science. The existing SS-FSL methods often rely on self-supervised learning (SSL) frameworks developed for vision or language, which assume the availability of a natural form of data augmentations. For tabular data, defining meaningful augmentations is non-trivial and can easily distort semantics, limiting the effectiveness of conventional SSL. In this work, we rethink SSL for tabular data and propose Separated-at-Birth Alignment (SeBA), a joint-embedding framework for SS-FSL that eliminates the dependence on augmentations. Our core idea is to separate the data into two independent, but complementary views and align the representations of one view to mirror the nearest-neighbor correspondence of the data in the second view. Our experimental evaluation supported by a theoretical analysis justifies that SeBA generates an output space, which improves the feature-label relationship. An experimental study conducted in various benchmark datasets demonstrates that SeBA achieves the state-of-the-art performance in the majority of cases, opening a new avenue for SS-FSL paradigm in the domain of tabular data.

</details>


### [83] [FlashEvolve: Accelerating Agent Self-Evolution with Asynchronous Stage Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08520)
*Zhengding Hu,Mingge Lu,Zhen Wang,Jixuan Ruan,Chang Chen,Zaifeng Pan,Yue Guan,Ruiyi Wang,Zhongkai Yu,Chao Zhang,Yufei Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlashEvolve是一个异步框架，通过解耦同步执行、处理异步带来的数据陈旧性问题（利用语言空间的可检查可修复特性）、并结合推测性阶段完成和自适应工作流控制，将LLM-based evolution的提案吞吐量提升3.5-4.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-based evolution虽然能通过优化非参数化工件来改进智能体，但其wall-clock成本是主要瓶颈，这种成本源于同步的阶段执行以及每个LLM-heavy阶段内部的不平衡。

Method: 提出FlashEvolve框架，核心方法包括：(1) 用异步工作器和队列替代同步执行，使不同阶段和步骤可以重叠；(2) 跟踪工件版本并采用不同的策略来更新、丢弃或修补陈旧工件，利用语言空间中陈旧工件的可读性，让LLM可以反思、修正并将其转化为有用的进化信号；(3) 引入推测性阶段完成和自适应工作流控制来提升吞吐量和token效率。

Result: 在GEPA工作负载上，FlashEvolve在本地vLLM上实现3.5倍的提案吞吐量提升，在API服务上实现4.9倍的提升；该设计同样适用于ACE和Meta-Harness。

Conclusion: 异步化设计可以有效解决LLM-based evolution的wall-clock成本瓶颈，且语言空间的陈旧性可以被利用为有价值信号而非仅仅延迟负担。

Abstract: LLM-based evolution has emerged as a promising way to improve agents by refining non-parametric artifacts, but its wall-clock cost remains a major bottleneck. We identify that this cost comes from synchronized stage execution and imbalance inside each LLM-heavy stage. We present FlashEvolve, an efficient framework that replaces synchronized execution with asynchronous workers and queues, allowing different stages and steps to overlap. To handle data staleness introduced by asynchrony, FlashEvolve tracks artifact versions and applies different policies to update, discard, or patch stale artifacts. Unlike weight-space staleness in asynchronous RL, language-space staleness is inspectable and repairable: a stale artifact is not just delayed work, but readable evidence that the LLM can reflect on, revise, and turn into useful evolution signal. FlashEvolve further improves throughput and token efficiency with speculative stage completion and adaptive workflow control. On GEPA workloads, FlashEvolve improves proposal throughput by $3.5\times$ on local vLLM and $4.9\times$ on API serving over synchronous GEPA. The same design also applies to ACE and Meta-Harness.

</details>


### [84] [Skill-CMIB: Multimodal Agent Skill for Consistent Action via Conditional Multimodal Information Bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08526)
*Zihan Huang,Junda Wu,Tong Yu,Qianqi Yan,Rohan Surana,Uttaran Bhattacharya,Lina Yao,Xin Eric Wang,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出条件多模态信息瓶颈（CMIB）方法，通过将多模态技能分解为可解释的文字技能卡和条件化的感知残差，减少跨模态冗余，提升LLM智能体在多模态任务中的执行一致性，且无需推理时多次采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在执行长序列动作时跨试验不一致，现有方法（如自一致性）依赖昂贵的多次采样推理，而内化策略无法分离可语言化的技能内容与感知残差信息。

Method: 提出条件多模态信息瓶颈（CMIB），通过联合瓶颈导出精确的序列分解：(1)文本阶段瓶颈蒸馏可解释的技能卡片；(2)条件多模态瓶颈压缩文本之外仍有预测能力的感知残差信息。采用变分目标优化条件分解。

Result: 获得可复用的多模态技能，提升执行稳定性，且无需多采样推理开销。

Conclusion: CMIB有效分离了可语言化技能内容与感知残差信息，结构性地减少了跨模态冗余，为多模态技能构建提供了高效且可靠的方案。

Abstract: While LLM-based agents excel at planning and executing long action sequences, their execution often remains inconsistent across trials, limiting reliability. Consolidating agent consistency requires distilling trial-error trajectories into reusable skills that preserve task-relevant invariants while discarding trajectory-specific noise. However, in multimodal settings, the key challenge is not only that useful invariants are distributed across vision and language information, but that different modalities support different kinds of reusable skill content: while some skills are verbalizable and interpretable, others reside in perceptual evidence beyond text. Text-only skills may lose perceptual cues, whereas storing text and perception naively introduces redundancy and noise. Existing inference-time methods, such as self-consistency, improve reliability through costly multi-sample decoding, while internalization strategies lack a way to separate verbalizable skill content from residual perceptual information. To address this, we introduce Conditional Multimodal Information Bottleneck (CMIB), a method for multimodal skill construction. CMIB begins with a joint bottleneck over multimodal skills and derives an exact sequential decomposition: (1) a text-stage bottleneck distilling interpretable skill cards, and (2) a conditional multimodal bottleneck compressing only residual information in perception that remains predictive beyond text. Unlike naive two-stream formulations, CMIB explicitly conditions the multimodal latent on the text skill, thus structurally reducing cross-modal redundancy and enabling independent control over textual and perceptual compression. We instantiate CMIB with a variational objective that makes its conditional decomposition tractable to optimize, yielding reusable multimodal skills that improve execution stability without incurring multi-sample inference overhead.

</details>


### [85] [Continuity Laws for Sequential Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08539)
*Annan Yu,Dongwei Lyu,N. Benjamin Erichson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了序列建模中一个被忽视的归纳偏置——时间连续性。通过形式化模型连续性（在时间细化下预测收敛到连续轨迹），发现S4表现出稳定的连续行为，而S6（Mamba核心）对输入振幅和选择性动力学更敏感。作者引入量化数据集连续性的指标，发现任务连续性、模型连续性与模型性能之间存在明显对齐。此外，连续性还支持简单的时间子采样策略，提升效率和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究序列模型中基于连续时间公式的模型（如状态空间模型）是否真正表现出时间连续性，以及这种连续性是否能转化为在具有连续时间结构任务上的更好性能。

Method: 形式化定义模型连续性为时间细化下的收敛性；引入量化数据集连续性的指标；通过实验分析任务连续性、模型连续性与性能之间的对齐关系；提出基于连续性的时间子采样策略。

Result: S4表现出稳定的连续行为；S6（Mamba核心）对输入振幅和选择性动力学更敏感；任务连续性、模型连续性与模型性能之间存在明确的实证对齐；连续性使简单的时间子采样策略能够同时提升效率和性能。

Conclusion: 时间连续性是一种重要的归纳偏置，不仅影响模型行为，还具有实际应用价值（如提升效率）。模型设计应重视连续性属性。

Abstract: Inductive biases influence the behavior and performance of sequential models. In this work, we study an underexplored inductive bias in sequential modeling: continuity in time. We ask a simple question: do models motivated by continuous-time formulations, such as state-space models, actually behave continuously in time, and does this translate into better performance on tasks with continuous temporal structure? To answer this, we formalize model continuity as convergence under temporal refinement, where a model is continuous if its predictions approach an underlying continuous trajectory as the temporal discretization is refined. We show that S4 exhibits stable continuous behavior, whereas S6 (the core of Mamba) can be more sensitive to input amplitude and selective dynamics, despite being derived from a continuous dynamical system. To study whether this distinction matters for learning, we also need a corresponding notion of task continuity. We therefore introduce a metric to quantify the continuity of datasets directly from their temporal structure. Across benchmarks, we find a clear empirical alignment between task continuity, model continuity, and model performance. Beyond an inductive bias, continuity also has practical consequences: we show that it enables a simple temporal subsampling strategy that improves both efficiency and performance.

</details>


### [86] [Tokens-per-Parameter Coverage Is Critical for Robust LLM Scaling Law Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08541)
*Joshua Shay Kricheli,Alexander Lawrence Reid,Soumajyoti Sarkar,Venkata Gandikota,Paulo Shakarian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有神经缩放定律研究普遍采用固定 tokens-per-parameter (TPP) 比例 k（即 D=kN）的共线性设计，导致 Gauss-Newton 最小二乘问题产生病态条件：当 N 和 D 的指数接近相等时，设计矩阵条件数以指数差平方的倒数增长，尺度系数几乎不可识别，置信区间膨胀一个数量级以上，模型在训练射线外的外推性能急剧下降。非共线性设计在四种定律、五个语料库上以 97.3% 胜率优于共线性设计，且该退化源于 Jacobian 几何，与损失函数具体形式无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示固定 TPP 比例（即 D=kN）的共线性设计在神经缩放定律估计中引入的病态条件问题，说明该设计导致参数不可识别和外推性能退化，并呼吁采用非共线性设计以获得可靠的缩放定律估计。

Method: 理论分析四种缩放定律形式下 Gauss-Newton 最小二乘的条件数，证明其以 (α-β)^(-2) 增长（α,β 分别为 N 和 D 的指数）；推导 TPP 多样性阈值的闭式解；跨四种定律、五个语料库、多种浮点精度进行实证比较共线性 vs 非共线性设计；从 Jacobian 几何角度证明退化根源与损失函数无关。

Result: 共线性设计导致条件数随指数差平方倒数增长，尺度系数不可识别；非共线性设计在 97.3% 的对比中优于共线性设计；证明了退化源于 Jacobian 几何，任何平滑估计目标只要曲率涉及 Jacobian 都会继承相同的病态条件。

Conclusion: 固定 TPP 比例的共线性设计是神经缩放定律估计中病态条件的根本原因，应避免使用；非共线性设计是获得可靠、可外推缩放定律的必要条件。

Abstract: Neural scaling laws approximate a language model's loss as a power-law function of parameter count $N$ and token count $D$. Following Chinchilla-style compute-optimal training, many studies fit scaling laws from runs performed under a fixed tokens-per-parameter (TPP) ratio $k$ and set $D = kN$. We show that this collinear design, combined with the empirically common near-equality of the exponents governing $N$ and $D$, induces an inherent ill-conditioning in the Gauss-Newton least-squares problem: the condition number of the design grows as the inverse square of the gap between the $N$ and $D$-exponents. The scale coefficients become practically unidentifiable, with confidence intervals inflating by an order of magnitude or more, yielding a ``sloppy'' model whose extrapolations degrade sharply off the training ray. We prove this for four scaling-law formalisms and derive a closed-form TPP-diversity threshold that is necessary and sufficient for well-conditioned estimation. Empirically, non-collinear designs outperform collinear ones on held-out splits with a 97.3\% win rate across four laws, five corpora, multiple floating point precision modes. We further show the degeneracy is rooted in Jacobian geometry and is not an artifact of the loss function: any smooth estimation objective whose curvature involves the Jacobian inherits the same ill-conditioning.

</details>


### [87] [A Call to Lagrangian Action: Learning Population Mechanics from Temporal Snapshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08550)
*Vincent Guan,Lazar Atanackovic,Kirill Neklyudov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Wasserstein拉格朗日力学（WLM），一种基于阻尼Wasserstein拉格朗日量的二阶动力学框架，并开发了首个能从观测边际分布中学习该动力学且无需指定拉格朗日量的算法，在涡旋动力学、胚胎发育和集群行为等多种动态中优于现有梯度流和流匹配方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Wasserstein梯度流的群体动力学模型只能最小化自由能，无法捕捉周期性等重要动力学特性，亟需一种能描述更丰富动力学行为的框架。

Method: 提出阻尼Wasserstein拉格朗日量下的最小化群体作用量观点，推导对应的哈密顿运动方程，形式化Wasserstein拉格朗日力学；并设计WLM算法，直接从观测边际分布学习二阶动力学，无需预先指定拉格朗日量。

Result: WLM在涡旋动力学、胚胎发育和集群行为等多种动力学任务中，在预测和插值未见边际分布方面均优于现有梯度流和流匹配方法。

Conclusion: Wasserstein拉格朗日力学为群体动力学建模提供了一个统一的二阶动力学框架，兼具经典力学、量子力学和梯度流的结构，WLM算法有效拓展了数据驱动的群体动力学建模能力。

Abstract: The population dynamics of molecules, cells, and organisms are governed by a number of unknown forces. In the last decade, population dynamics have predominantly been modeled with Wasserstein gradient flows. However, since gradient flows minimize free energy, they fail to capture important dynamical properties, such as periodicity. In this work, we propose a change in perspective by considering dynamics that minimize a population-level action under a damped Wasserstein Lagrangian. By deriving the corresponding Hamiltonian equations of motion, we formalize Wasserstein Lagrangian Mechanics, a structured class of second-order dynamics that encompasses classical mechanics, quantum mechanics, and gradient flows. We then propose WLM as the first algorithm that learns these second-order dynamics from observed marginals, without specifying the Lagrangian. By directly learning the population mechanics, WLM can both forecast and interpolate unseen marginals, and outperforms existing gradient flow and flow matching methods across a wide range of dynamics, including vortex dynamics, embryonic development, and flocking.

</details>


### [88] [Can Revealed Preferences Clarify LLM Alignment and Steering?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08556)
*Khurram Yamin,Jingjing Tang,Eric Horvitz,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过显示偏好分析来估计LLM隐含偏好（成本函数）的实证流程，并在四个医疗诊断领域评估了多个前沿和开源模型，发现模型虽有内部一致性，但在忠实地报告或采纳用户指定的偏好方面存在显著缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被越来越多地用于高 stakes 不确定性决策，其对齐不仅依赖事实准确性，还取决于模型如何权衡不同结果之间的取舍，因此需要系统的方法来评估模型的隐含偏好及其与用户目标的一致性。

Method: 首先获取模型对未知变量的概率分布及其在决策任务中做出的选择，然后拟合离散选择模型来恢复最能理性化模型决策的成本函数（即显示偏好）。

Result: 许多模型具有非平凡的内部一致性，但在忠实地报告偏好（模型能口头描述的目标是否与揭示的决策策略匹配）以及采纳用户指定的偏好（通过提示能否可靠地将策略导向用户指定的成本函数）方面存在显著弱点。

Conclusion: 显示偏好分析框架可以严格评估LLM决策的目标导向一致性、自我报告偏好的真实性以及通过提示进行偏好引导的可靠性，目前模型在这些方面仍有不足。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used to make or support high-stakes decisions under uncertainty, where alignment depends not only on factual accuracy but on how models weigh tradeoffs between different outcomes. We present an empirical pipeline for estimating the implied preferences that an LLM's observed choices optimize: we elicit the model's probability distribution over unknowns along with the choice it would make for the decision task and then fit a discrete choice model to recover the cost function that best rationalizes the model's decisions. We show how this revealed-preference description allows rigorous evaluation of whether models behave in a consistently goal-directed way, whether they can verbalize a description of their objectives which matches their revealed decision policy, and whether prompting can reliably steer those policies to implement a user-specified cost function. We apply this evaluation across four medical diagnosis domains and multiple frontier and open-source models. We find that while many models have a nontrivial degree of internal coherence, they also have significant weaknesses in faithfully reporting or adopting preferences in response to user direction.

</details>


### [89] [Finer is Better (with the Right Scaling)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08565)
*Clemens Schaefer,Gil Tabak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了标准abs-max量化缩放在块尺寸减小时导致模型质量下降的悖论，发现其根源在于FP4元素格式的粗糙上量化箱与重尾张量分布的不良交互，而非细粒度缩放本身的固有缺陷。通过防止缩放因子下溢为零、采用4-over-6等算法干预，可在标准硬件兼容格式（OCP E4M3）下达到与自定义宽指数格式（UE5M3）相当的性能，从而彻底解决了块尺寸悖论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有文献发现标准abs-max缩放随块尺寸减小反而导致模型质量下降，形成悖论，本文旨在探究这一现象的底层机制并给出解决方案。

Method: 通过分析重尾张量分布与FP4格式量化箱的交互机制，提出三个层面的干预：防止缩放因子下溢为零、4-over-6方法校正量化几何、暴力搜索建立最优基线。

Result: 证明在正确算法配方下，标准硬件兼容格式（OCP E4M3）可匹配自定义宽指数格式（UE5M3）的性能，并在多个大语言模型上验证了困惑度的改善。

Conclusion: 块尺寸悖论并非细粒度缩放的固有问题，而是可以通过算法干预完全解决；标准格式与自定义格式之间存在有价值的可互换性。

Abstract: Microscaling is a critical technique for preserving the quality of Large Language Models (LLMs) quantized to ultra-low precision formats. Intuitively, finer block sizes should yield lower quantization error; however, a paradox recently identified in the literature demonstrates that standard abs-max scaling can actually degrade model quality as block sizes shrink. In this work, we investigate the underlying mechanics of this phenomenon. We demonstrate that this degradation is not an inherent limitation of finer granularity, but is primarily driven by heavy-tailed tensor distributions interacting poorly with the coarse upper quantization bins of the FP4 element format. Specifically, we show that i) preventing the scaling factor from underflowing to zero mitigates localized errors, ii) targeted algorithmic interventions like the 4-over-6 methodology effectively correct the quantization geometry for large elements, and iii) a brute-force search establishes an optimal baseline, confirming that the theoretical Mean Squared Error (MSE) strictly improves with finer block sizes. Ultimately, our findings reveal a valuable interchangeability: applying the correct algorithmic recipe allows standard, hardware-compliant formats (like OCP E4M3) to match the performance of custom, wider-exponent formats (like UE5M3). We validate these results across several large language models, fully resolving the block size paradox and achieving robust downstream perplexity improvements.

</details>


### [90] [Different Prompts, Different Ranks: Prompt-aware Dynamic Rank Selection for SVD-based LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08568)
*Hengyi Zhu,Zhendong Mi,Grace Li Zhang,Shaoyi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PARSE提出了一种提示感知的秩选择框架，通过离线训练的线性路由器为不同输入动态选择最优的SVD压缩秩，并结合模式缓存和内核融合提升效率，在多个SVD方法上显著提升准确率和推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SVD压缩方法采用静态秩截断，对所有输入使用固定的前缀奇异组件，无法适应不同提示的最优秩差异，且对校准集选择敏感，导致性能次优。

Method: 离线训练线性路由器，以大规模语料上密集模型输出为监督信号，实现提示感知的秩选择；利用语义相似提示共享秩选择模式的特性，通过模式缓存和专家内存聚合、内核融合提升系统效率。

Result: 在LLaMA-7B压缩比0.6下，平均任务准确率提升最高10%；相比原生SVD执行，prefill加速最高2.5倍，decode加速最高2.4倍。

Conclusion: PARSE与现有SVD方法正交，能同时提升模型质量和推理效率，为动态秩选择提供了一种高效且实用的后训练压缩方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have rapidly grown in scale, creating substantial memory and computational costs that hinder efficient deployment. Singular value decomposition (SVD) has emerged as an effective post-training compression technique, but existing SVD-based methods rely on static rank truncation, applying a fixed prefix of singular components to all inputs regardless of their diversity. We identify two limitations of this static design: the optimal rank varies across individual prompts, and the selected rank is sensitive to the choice of calibration set, leading to suboptimal performance across diverse inputs. To address these challenges, we propose $\textbf{PARSE}$, a post-training framework for $\textbf{P}$rompt-$\textbf{A}$ware $\textbf{R}$ank $\textbf{S}$election as $\textbf{E}$xperts in SVD-compressed LLMs. PARSE trains a linear router offline to perform prompt-aware rank selection, decoupling it from calibration information by supervising the router against dense-model outputs on a large-scale corpus. We further observe that rank-selection patterns are shared across semantically similar prompts and remain stable across decoding steps, allowing appropriate rank subsets to be served directly from a pattern cache at inference. Complemented by expert memory aggregation and kernel fusion for system-level efficiency, PARSE is orthogonal to existing SVD-based pipelines and consistently improves both model quality and inference efficiency. Integrated with four representative SVD-based methods, PARSE improves average task accuracy by up to 10% at a compression ratio of 0.6 on LLaMA-7B, and achieves up to 2.5 $\times$ prefill and 2.4 $\times$ decode speedup over native SVD execution.

</details>


### [91] [Uncovering Intra-expert Activation Sparsity for Efficient Mixture-of-Expert Model Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08575)
*Jongseok Park,Sunga Kim,Zhenyu Gu,Ion Stoica,Alvin Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索了混合专家（MoE）模型中专家内部激活稀疏性的潜力，发现现有预训练MoE模型在不修改激活函数或参数的情况下，每个专家内可提供高达90%的稀疏性。通过在vLLM的MoE执行流水线中跳过非活跃神经元的计算，实现了MoE层最高2.5倍加速，端到端1.2倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构已成为大语言模型的标准，但通过更细粒度专家实现稀疏性因专家崩溃和负载不均等训练挑战变得愈发困难。本文探索了被忽视的专家内部激活稀疏性作为MoE模型中稀疏性的互补维度。

Method: 利用预训练MoE模型中已有的专家内部激活稀疏性，在vLLM的MoE执行流水线中跳过非活跃神经元的计算，无需修改激活函数或模型参数。

Result: 在8个从1B到400B参数的MoE模型上，每个专家内提供高达90%稀疏性而无显著精度损失；MoE层执行加速最高2.5倍，端到端加速1.2倍。

Conclusion: 专家内部激活稀疏性是MoE模型中一种有效且互补的稀疏性维度，可在现有优化基础上进一步提升推理效率。

Abstract: Mixture of Experts (MoE) architecture has become the standard for state-of-the-art large language models, owing to its computational efficiency through sparse expert activation. However, sparsity through finer expert granularity is becoming increasingly difficult to achieve due to fundamental training challenges such as expert collapse and load imbalance. In this work, we explore and leverage intra-expert activation sparsity as a complementary and underexplored dimension of sparsity in MoE models. Surprisingly, substantial intra-expert sparsity is readily available in existing pre-trained MoE models, without any modification to the activation function or model parameters, providing up to 90% sparsity within each expert without significant accuracy loss. We explore intra-expert activation sparsity across eight off-the-shelf MoE models ranging from 1B to 400B parameters, and extend the MoE execution pipeline of vLLM to leverage intra-expert activation sparsity by skipping the computations of inactive neurons, on top of its existing optimizations, achieving up to 2.5 times speedup in MoE layer execution and 1.2 times end-to-end speedup compared to the original dense vLLM baseline.

</details>


### [92] [Probing the Impact of Scale on Data-Efficient, Generalist Transformer World Models for Atari](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08578)
*Jooyeon Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过最小化Transformer世界模型在Atari 100k基准上的实验，揭示了环境内在的缩放机制差异：单个任务中存在单调改进与退化两种截然不同的缩放行为；联合训练（26个环境）能稳定缩放动态，确保所有环境单调提升；改进的世界模型保真度可直接转化为下游控制性能（中位标准化分数0.770）。研究指出未来进展既需要架构创新，也依赖于精确的缩放策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型研究常混淆架构机制与模型规模（scale）的独立影响，缺乏对缩放行为本身如何受环境特性约束的系统分析。

Method: 使用最小化Transformer世界模型，在Atari 100k基准上基于预设专家策略的固定离线数据集，分别分析单任务和统一多任务（26个环境）下的缩放行为，并评估下游控制性能。

Result: 1) 单任务中环境呈现两种缩放机制：部分可越过插值阈值实现过参数化单调改进，部分陷入经典区间（更大模型降低保真度）；2) 联合训练稳定了缩放动态，所有环境均实现单调增益；3) 学习于模拟动态的策略达到0.770中位专家-随机标准化分数。

Conclusion: 世界模型的未来进步不仅依赖于架构创新，同样取决于针对环境特性的精确缩放策略。

Abstract: Developing generalist systems that retain human-like data efficiency is a central challenge. While world models (WMs) offer a promising path, existing research often conflates architectural mechanisms with the independent impact of model \emph{scale}. In this work, we use a minimalist transformer world model to analyze scaling behaviors on the Atari 100k benchmark, using fixed offline datasets derived from a presupposed expert policy. Our results reveal that environments fundamentally fall into distinct scaling regimes, even when constrained by identical offline data budgets and model capacities. For individual tasks, some environments naturally allow models to pass the interpolation threshold, yielding monotonic improvements in the overparameterized regime, while others remain trapped in the classical regime, where larger world models degrade fidelity. In the unified setting, i.e., a single transformer trained on a suite of 26 Atari environments, we uncover that joint training stabilizes scaling dynamics, ensuring monotonic gains across all environments, regardless of their distinct inherent scaling regimes. Finally, we demonstrate that improved fidelity translates directly to downstream control, with policies learned entirely within the simulated dynamics achieving a median expert-random-normalized score of 0.770. Our findings suggest that future progress lies as much in precise scaling strategies as in architectural innovation.

</details>


### [93] [Kaczmarz Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08587)
*Jiaxuan Zou,Ruifeng Ren,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kaczmarz线性注意力（KLA），通过将DeltaNet中经验学习的更新系数替换为基于Kaczmarz投影法推导的键范数归一化动态步长，在保持状态形状、门控和线性递归不变的前提下，实现了更优的困惑度、长上下文检索能力和解码吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gated DeltaNet（GDN）中用于平衡遗忘和更新幅度的系数是经验学习的，而非从底层目标函数推导得出，这可能导致次优的更新幅度。

Method: 重新审视GDN背后的在线回归目标，受Kaczmarz投影法启发，推导出键范数归一化的动态步长 β_t = η_t / (||k_t||_2^2 + ε) 用于残差更新；提出KLA，仅需修改GDN的一个标量，保持状态形状、门控、线性递归和分块并行算法不变。

Result: 在0.4B规模、1B token预算下，KLA验证困惑度8.09（GDN为8.50），65K token保持稳定；单针检索100%准确率，8路多查询关联召回比GDN提升7.03个百分点，32K上下文解码吞吐量提升2.1倍。

Conclusion: 键范数归一化的Kaczmarz系数是delta-rule序列模型的一阶设计轴，能在不改变循环状态或硬件核的前提下提升准确性、外推能力和解码效率。

Abstract: Long-context language modeling remains central to modern sequence modeling, but the quadratic cost of Transformer attention makes scaling computationally prohibitive. Linear recurrent models address this bottleneck by compressing the context into a fixed-size state, making the rule that forgets, writes, and edits information a central design problem. To address state maintenance, Gated DeltaNet (GDN) combines gated state decay with delta-rule residual writes, using a learnable coefficient to balance forgetting and update magnitude. However, this coefficient is learned empirically rather than derived from the underlying objective, which can lead to suboptimal update magnitudes. We revisit the online-regression objective underlying GDN and, inspired by the Kaczmarz projection method, derive the key-norm-normalized dynamic step size $β_t = η_t / (\|k_t\|_2^2 + ε)$ for residual updates. We propose Kaczmarz Linear Attention (KLA), a one-scalar modification of GDN that preserves the state shape, gates, linear recurrence, and chunkwise parallel algorithm. At the 0.4B scale with a 1B-token budget, KLA achieves the lowest validation perplexity among evaluated linear-time baselines, 8.09 versus 8.50 for GDN, and remains stable up to 65K tokens. On controlled tasks, KLA reaches 100% on single-needle-in-a-haystack retrieval, improves 8x multi-query associative recall by 7.03 points over GDN, and delivers 2.1x higher decode throughput at 32K context. These results suggest that the key-norm-normalized Kaczmarz coefficient is a first-order design axis for delta-rule sequence models: it improves accuracy, extrapolation, and decoding efficiency without changing the recurrent state or hardware kernel.

</details>


### [94] [Lattice Deduction Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08605)
*Liam Davis,Leopold Haller,Alberto Alfarano,Mark Santolucito*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Lattice Deduction Transformer (LDT)，一种通过格投影实现逻辑推理的递归Transformer，在Sudoku-Extreme、Snowflake Sudoku和Maze-Hard上取得高精度，而前沿大语言模型得分为0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小型递归推理器训练成本高，而大型语言模型在结构化逻辑推理任务上表现极差（0%准确率），需要一种高效、可靠且可验证的神经推理方法。

Method: LDT采用递归Transformer架构，在每轮前向传播之间将隐态通过格（lattice）进行投影，模拟逻辑演绎过程；通过基于抽象解释（abstract interpretation）的域无关近似监督训练，训练过程类似约束求解器中的搜索式演绎。

Result: 80万参数的LDT在Sudoku-Extreme和Snowflake Sudoku上达到100%准确率，180万参数变体在Maze-Hard上达到99.9%准确率，训练成本远低于先前的小型递归推理器。模型具有经验可靠性（返回正确答案或放弃作答）。前沿LLM（如GPT-4等）在这三个基准上得分为0%。

Conclusion: LDT证明了在极低参数规模和训练成本下，通过将神经架构与形式化推理结构（格）结合，可以实现超越大规模语言模型的精确逻辑推理能力，为神经符号推理提供了有效方向。

Abstract: We introduce the Lattice Deduction Transformer (LDT), a recurrent transformer that approximates logically sound deduction by projecting its latent state through a lattice between forward passes. We train on-policy in a process that mirrors deduction in a search-based constraint solver and supervise training via a domain-agnostic, abstract-interpretation-based approximation of the set of solution candidates. An $800$K-parameter LDT achieves $100\%$ accuracy on Sudoku-Extreme and Snowflake Sudoku, at a fraction of the training cost of prior small recurrent reasoners, while remaining empirically sound: the model returns a correct answer or abstains. A $1.8$M-parameter variant reaches $99.9\%$ accuracy on Maze-Hard. Frontier LLMs score $0\%$ on all three benchmarks.

</details>


### [95] [Reasoning-Aware Training for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08625)
*Md Atik Ahamed,Mihir Parmar,Palash Goyal,Chun-Liang Li,Qiang Cheng,Tomas Pfister,Jinsung Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STRIDE通过将轻量级LLM的语义推理蒸馏为连续嵌入先验，无缝注入到时间序列基础模型的数值编码器中，在保持高精度预测的同时赋予模型可解释的定性推理能力，在GIFT-Eval和TFRBench上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）虽在数值预测上表现优异，但缺乏定性推理能力；而直接应用LLM处理时序数据存在模态鸿沟——文本分词器会割裂连续数值，破坏数学关系并导致序列长度爆炸，带来巨大的计算开销。

Method: STRIDE框架包含：(1) 将推理轨迹蒸馏到轻量级LLM中；(2) 对LLM隐藏状态进行均值池化后，作为跨模态先验动态投影到目标数值编码器；(3) 联合优化交叉熵损失和分位数损失。

Result: 在GIFT-Eval上达到SOTA数值预测性能（MASE 0.674, CRPS 0.454）；在TFRBench上展现优越的域内、域外数值预测及推理性能；作为即插即用增强模块，能一致提升多种TSFMs（如Chronos-2, Timer-S1）的性能。

Conclusion: 将语义推理作为连续先验注入TSFMs，不仅赋予了模型人类可解释的推理能力，还从根本上提升了预测精度，有效弥合了数值预测与语言推理之间的模态鸿沟。

Abstract: Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) excel at numerical forecasting but operate as black boxes lacking qualitative reasoning. Conversely, applying LLMs directly to temporal data introduces a modality gap: text tokenizers fragment continuous numerical values, degrading mathematical relationships and exploding sequence lengths, leading to computational overhead. To resolve this, we introduce STRIDE (Strategic Time-series Reasoning Injected via Distilled Embeddings), a novel framework natively integrating LLM reasoning into the continuous embedding space of TSFMs. Instead of discrete tokens, STRIDE distills reasoning traces into a lightweight LLM, dynamically projecting its mean-pooled hidden states as a cross-modal prior into the target numerical encoder. The architecture is jointly optimized using cross-entropy and quantile losses. Evaluations demonstrate STRIDE establishes state-of-the-art numerical forecasting on GIFT-Eval (0.674 MASE, 0.454 CRPS) compared to TSFMs and exhibits superior in-domain and out-of-domain numerical as well as reasoning performance on TFRBench. Specifically, STRIDE acts as a plug-and-play enhancement, consistently improving diverse TSFMs (e.g., Chronos-2, Timer-S1) across various LLM configurations. Thus, injecting semantic reasoning as a continuous prior equips TSFMs with human-interpretable reasoning while fundamentally improving predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [96] [PAAC: Privacy-Aware Agentic Device-Cloud Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08646)
*Liangqi Yuan,Wenzhi Fang,Shiqiang Wang,Christopher G. Brinton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PAAC 是一个隐私感知的智能体框架，通过将规划器-执行器分解与设备-云端边界对齐，使角色专业化本身成为隐私机制，在三个智能体基准测试中平均准确率提升15-36%，泄漏减少2-6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体面临结构性矛盾：云端智能体推理能力强但暴露用户数据，设备端智能体保护隐私但能力受限。现有设备-云端设计将边界视为计算拆分而非信任边界，且现有净化器在策略灵活性与工具调用所需的结构保真度之间难以兼顾。

Method: PAAC将规划器-执行器分解与设备-云端边界对齐：云端智能体对类型化占位符令牌进行推理，保留敏感值的推理角色但丢弃内容；设备端智能体识别敏感片段并将执行结果蒸馏为关键发现。净化过程由设备端LLM提议屏蔽哪些片段，确定性注册表执行所有替换和还原操作，确保动作可直接在设备上执行。

Result: 在三个智能体基准测试的严格隐私设置下，PAAC主导了隐私-准确率帕累托前沿，平均准确率提升15-36%，泄漏减少2-6倍，在固定实体分类之外的隐私目标上提升最大。在涵盖数学、科学、金融等10个领域的17个额外基准测试中均有一致改进。

Conclusion: 通过将角色专业化作为隐私机制，PAAC有效解决了LLM智能体在隐私与能力之间的结构性矛盾，实现了隐私保护与任务准确性的双赢。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents face a structural tension: cloud agents provide strong reasoning but expose user data, while on-device agents preserve privacy at the cost of overall capability. Existing device-cloud designs treat this boundary as a compute split rather than a trust boundary suited to agentic workloads, and existing sanitizers force a choice between policy flexibility and the structural fidelity tool calls require. In this work, we develop PAAC, a privacy-aware agentic framework that aligns planner--executor decomposition with the device-cloud boundary so that role specialization itself becomes the privacy mechanism. The cloud agent reasons over typed placeholder tokens that preserve each sensitive value's reasoning role while discarding its content, while the on-device agent identifies sensitive spans and distills each step's execution outcome into compact key findings. Sanitization confines the on-device LLM to proposing which spans to mask, while a deterministic registry performs all substitution and reversal, keeping actions directly executable on device. On three agentic benchmarks under strict privacy settings, PAAC dominates the Pareto frontier of privacy and accuracy, improving average accuracy by 15-36\% and reducing average leakage by 2-6$\times$ over state-of-the-art device-cloud baselines, with the largest margins on privacy targets outside fixed entity taxonomies. We find consistent improvements on 17 additional benchmarks spanning 10 domains, including math, science, and finance.

</details>


### [97] [FLUX: Geometry-Aware Longitudinal Flow Matching with Mixture of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08648)
*Josue Ortega Caro,Yongxu Zhang,Hannah M Batchelor,Sizhuang He,Jessica Cardin,Shreya Saxena*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FLUX是一个几何感知的纵向流匹配框架，用于从非配对纵向快照数据中联合建模传输过程和无监督发现潜在状态转换机制，通过学习数据依赖的度量构建几何感知的条件路径，并利用混合专家模型分解速度场来识别隐式状态切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多生物系统在潜在状态之间切换（如学习、刺激上下文、内部状态或发育阶段），但观测数据仅为非配对的纵向快照（同一细胞/神经元/动物未跟踪匹配轨迹），导致两个耦合挑战：轨迹需尊重嵌入高维测量的低维流形，模型需识别传输机制何时变化。

Method: 提出FLUX框架：1) 学习数据依赖的度量（整合标注和未标注观测）；2) 利用该度量构建几何感知的条件路径连接相邻边际分布；3) 通过Straight-Through Gumbel-Softmax路由将速度场分解为稀疏专家向量场。

Result: 在流形控制、状态切换Lorenz系统、宽场皮层钙成像（联想学习）和拟胚体单细胞分化等多个任务中，FLUX成功重建纵向传输并恢复可解释的状态结构。消融实验表明，单独使用混合专家路由不够：缺乏几何学习的FLUX可拟合局部传输，但在状态编码于局部动态时无法或弱化状态发现。

Conclusion: 几何感知的速度分解为从非配对纵向快照中发现潜在生物状态转换提供了通用策略。

Abstract: Many biological systems evolve through continuous local dynamics while switching between latent regimes defined by learning, stimulus context, internal state, or developmental stage. These processes are often observed only as unpaired longitudinal snapshots: the same cells, neurons, or animals are not tracked as matched trajectories, even though population states are sampled across successive stages. This creates two coupled challenges. First, trajectories must respect curved low-dimensional manifolds embedded in high-dimensional biological measurements. Second, the model must identify when the transport mechanism itself changes. We introduce FLUX (FLow matching for Unpaired longitudinal data with miXture-of-experts), a geometry-aware longitudinal flow-matching framework for joint transport modeling and unsupervised regime discovery. FLUX learns a data-dependent metric from pooled labeled and unlabeled observations, uses that metric to construct geometry-aware conditional paths between adjacent marginals, and decomposes the resulting velocity field into sparse expert vector fields selected by a Straight-Through Gumbel-Softmax router. Across manifold controls, a regime-switching Lorenz system, widefield cortical calcium imaging during associative learning, and embryoid body single-cell differentiation, FLUX reconstructs longitudinal transport while recovering interpretable regime structure. Ablations show that mixture-of-experts routing alone is insufficient: FLUX without geometric learning can fit local transport but fails or weakens regime discovery when regimes are encoded in local dynamics. These results suggest that geometry-aware velocity decomposition provides a general strategy for discovering latent biological state transitions from unpaired longitudinal snapshots.

</details>


### [98] [Sketch-and-Verify: Structured Inference-Time Scaling via Program Sketching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08658)
*Shan Jiang,Zijian Yi,Chenguang Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SKETCHVERIFY是一种同层成本-性能策略，通过让LLM枚举K种算法策略、为每种策略编写程序草稿（含??空洞）并填充M次，生成K×M个结构多样化的候选程序，再通过执行验证和指纹聚类选择最优解。在同层模型下，草图策略在匹配候选数量时显著优于传统平面采样；但跨层时，使用更强模型的基础贪心策略仍是最优选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决实践中因延迟、部署或预算限制只能使用小型廉价代码模型时，如何高效利用少量额外测试时计算资源的问题。传统平面采样生成大量候选但结构多样性不足，而草图策略能保证每个额外草图探索不同的算法路径。

Method: (1) LLM枚举K种不同的算法策略；(2) 为每种策略编写一个包含??空洞的程序草稿；(3) 对每个草稿填充M次，生成K×M个结构多样的候选程序；(4) 通过执行验证和指纹聚类进行筛选。

Result: 在HumanEval+的困难子集上：(1) Lite Sketch K=2,M=5恢复11/19(58%)，优于平面N=10的5/19(26%)，+32pp；(2) Lite Sketch K=10,M=10恢复15/19(79%)，优于平面N=100的10/19(53%)，+26pp；(3) 平面N=50仍落后于Sketch K=2,M=5，+11pp；(4) Pro贪心(89%)优于Lite Sketch K=10,M=10(79%)。

Conclusion: 在同层模型内，草图策略是比平面采样更高效的测试时计算分配方式；但草图策略无法替代模型升级——如果有更强的模型可用，应优先使用强模型的基础贪心策略。给出了同层优化与跨层升级的实用决策规则。

Abstract: SKETCHVERIFY is a within-tier cost-performance policy, not a universal accuracy improvement. The operational question: a practitioner stuck with a small, cheap code model (here, Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite) for latency, deployment, or budget reasons -- how should they spend a small amount of extra test-time compute? SKETCHVERIFY factorizes the search space: the LLM enumerates K distinct algorithmic strategies, writes a program sketch for each (a partial program with ?? holes), and fills each sketch M times, producing K x M structurally diverse candidates that are verified by execution and selected by fingerprint clustering. Each extra sketch is guaranteed to explore a different algorithm; each extra flat sample likely duplicates an existing one. Our central evidence is a cost-quality Pareto plot on HumanEval+ across three Gemini tiers (Lite, Flash, Pro), and a reanalysis of the 19 problems where Lite greedy fails. Two findings: (1) Within-tier, sketching dominates flat sampling at matched candidate count. On the hard subset, Lite Sketch K=2, M=5 recovers 11/19 (58%) vs. flat N=10 at 5/19 (26%, +32pp); Lite Sketch K=10, M=10 recovers 15/19 (79%) vs. flat N=100 at 10/19 (53%, +26pp). Flat cannot close the gap even at ~3x the budget: flat N=50 still loses to Sketch K=2, M=5 by +11pp. (2) Cross-tier, sketching does not replace upgrading. Pro greedy (89%) dominates Lite Sketch K=10, M=10 (79%) on both pass@1 and dollar cost. Practitioner rule: if a stronger tier is available, use greedy on it; otherwise sketching is the cost-effective way to spend extra compute. We characterize the K-vs-M trade-off via a Flash Lite scaling sweep, report HumanEval+ saturation on Flash and Pro, and show the method composes cleanly with execution-based selection from the concurrent Semantic Voting line of work.

</details>


### [99] [Optimised Support Vector Regression for California Housing Price Prediction: The Critical Role of Feature Engineering and Hyperparameter Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08660)
*Emmanuel Adutwum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过特征工程、缩放和超参数调优，将SVR在California Housing数据集上的R²从0.60提升至0.723（相对提升约20%），推翻SVR在该基准上表现最差的结论，并证明性能短板主要源于实验配置而非算法本身局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SVR在California Housing基准上被报告为表现最差的算法（R²=0.60），本文旨在验证该性能是否归因于实验配置选择（如特征缩放缺失、超参数未优化）而非SVR算法的固有缺陷。

Method: 构建10个领域驱动衍生特征；使用集成特征重要性筛选预测变量；在泄漏安全的scikit-learn Pipeline中结合3折交叉验证进行随机超参数搜索；通过四阶段消融研究（原始→缩放→特征工程→超参数调优）分离各组件贡献。

Result: 缩放贡献+0.744 R²（从-0.054到0.690），特征工程增加+0.026，超参数调优增加+0.008；最终测试R²=0.723（较0.60提升20%）；在10模型对比中排名第4；10折交叉验证平均R²=0.703（95% CI: [0.630, 0.775]）。

Conclusion: SVR性能差的主因是缺乏统一预处理流水线中的特征缩放，领域特征工程和系统超参数调优提供进一步增量收益。经适当配置后SVR可超越简单基线，性能接近集成方法。

Abstract: In the recent literature, Support Vector Regression (SVR) has been cited as one of the weakest performers on the California Housing benchmark dataset, with Preethi et al. (2025)specifically ranking it last among the algorithms they tested, reporting an R2 of only 0.60. This paper examines whether the previously reported performance reflects experimental configuration choices rather than an inherent algorithmic limitation. A structured experimental workflow is applied: ten domain-motivated derived features are constructed from the eight raw inputs, an exploratory ensemble feature importance analysis identifies the most predictive candidates, and a randomised search over hyperparameter combinations with three-fold cross-validation selects the optimal SVR configuration within a leakage-safe scikit-learn Pipeline. A formal four-stage ablation study isolates the contribution of each component: scaling alone accounts for +0.744 in R2 (from -0.054 to 0.690), feature engineering adds +0.026 (to 0.716), and hyperparameter tuning contributes +0.008 (to 0.723). The resulting tuned SVR achieves a test R2 of 0.723, a 0.123-point absolute improvement over the previously reported SVR result (from 0.60 to 0.723, approximately 20% relative gain). In the ten-model comparison, the tuned SVR ranks fourth with R2 = 0.723, below XGBoost (0.832), Random Forest (0.814) and Gradient Boosting (0.783), while substantially outperforming simpler baselines. Ten-fold cross-validation yields a mean R2 of 0.703 (95% CI: [0.630, 0.775]), confirming robust generalisation. The observed improvement from R2 = 0.60 to R2 = 0.723 is associated primarily with proper feature scaling within a unified preprocessing pipeline, with domain-motivated feature engineering and systematic hyperparameter tuning, providing further incremental gains.

</details>


### [100] [The Cancellation Hypothesis in Critic-Free RL: From Outcome Rewards to Token Credits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08666)
*Tianhao Cheng,Zeyu Huang,Zihan Qiu,Yu Cheng,Edoardo Ponti,Yinghui Xu,Ivan Titov,Zenglin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了无评论强化学习（critic-free RL）中的“token翻转”现象，即正负 rollout 中 token 概率变化比例相似；通过“耦合梯度交互”解释该现象，并提出“抵消假说”：正负 rollout 共享的 token 因耦合信号相互抵消，而成功 rollout 特有的 token 获得更强的强化，从而实现 token 级别的隐式信用分配。基于此设计了两种简化批处理干预方法，实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对无评论 RL（基于序列级奖励）的常见解释是它增强成功 rollout、惩罚失败 rollout，但这种视角停留在序列层面。本文旨在从 token 级别重新审视该过程，揭示其中的微观机制。

Method: 通过分析 token 概率变化，发现 token 翻转现象；利用梯度耦合分析解释该现象，提出抵消假说；设计 query-preserved mini-batching 和 reward-balanced batching 两种批处理干预方法进行验证。

Result: 正负 rollout 中 token 被增强或抑制的比例高度相似；耦合效应主要发生在预测置信度低的相同 token 之间；抵消假说得到实证支持：相比仅用正 rollout 训练，无评论 RL 将更新从模板/格式 token 转移到推理 token，且被增强的 token 始终比被抑制的 token 具有更高价值。两种干预方法在多个模型规模上提升了 RLVR 训练效果。

Conclusion: 抵消假说既作为解释原理也作为实用设计标准，对无评论 RL 训练具有指导意义。

Abstract: A commonly accepted explanation of critic-free RL for LLMs, based on sequence-level rewards, is that it reinforces successful rollouts with a positive advantage while penalizing failed ones. In contrast, we study critic-free RL from a token-level perspective, revealing the token-flipping phenomenon: positive and negative rollouts exhibit remarkably similar proportions of tokens whose probabilities are boosted or suppressed during RL training. To explain this phenomenon, we further show that a token's change in probability is not fully determined by its own advantage; coupled gradient interactions with other tokens also play a non-negligible role. Specifically, these token coupling effects occur primarily between identical tokens that are both predicted with low confidence. Building upon this analysis, we propose the cancellation hypothesis: as a result of coupling, opposing signals cancel out for tokens shared by positive and negative rollouts, while tokens more specific to successful rollouts receive stronger reinforcement, thereby inducing hidden token-level credit assignment from rollout-level rewards. We support this hypothesis with complementary empirical evidence. (1) Compared with training on only positive rollouts, critic-free RL shifts updates from template and formatting tokens toward reasoning tokens; (2) Tokens boosted by critic-free RL consistently demonstrate higher value than suppressed tokens, regardless of whether they originate from positive or negative rollouts. Guided by this view, we implement two batching interventions to encourage or preserve cancellation in critic-free RL training: query-preserved mini-batching and reward-balanced batching. Despite their simplicity, these interventions improve RLVR training across multiple model scales, supporting cancellation as both an explanatory principle and a practical design criterion for critic-free RL training.

</details>


### [101] [PHIDA: Persistence-Guided Node-to-Cluster Mapping for Online Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08673)
*Naoki Masuyama,Yusuke Nojima,Stefan Wermter,Yuichiro Toda,Hisao Ishibuchi,Chu Kiong Loo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PHIDA方法，利用持久同调约束的自适应共振理论在线聚类，将学习到的节点映射到聚类，解决了现有方法中节点到聚类映射隐式导致的结构敏感性问题。在24个基准数据集上取得最优平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线聚类方法中，节点学习通常是显式的，但从学习到的节点状态到输出聚类的映射往往隐式或简化，导致输出聚类对图中的弱桥接或距离敏感，缺乏对节点组在映射过程中保持完整性的显式约束。

Method: PHIDA结合了逆距离自适应共振理论（IDA）节点学习与持久同调（PH）约束的节点到聚类映射，通过PH约束保留原始PH成分，并在节点学习过程中利用PH成分视图。

Result: 在24个基准数据集上，PHIDA在静态比较中取得最佳平均排名，优于仅静态聚类方法；在非平稳设置中整体性能优于评估的在线方法。消融实验表明性能提升来自PH约束映射和PH成分视图的使用。

Conclusion: PH约束映射保留了原始PH成分，结合节点学习中的PH成分视图，有效提升了在线聚类的性能。

Abstract: Online clustering methods that adaptively create and update nodes as data arrive often make node learning explicit, whereas the mapping from the learned node state to output clusters often remains implicit or simplified. Implicit mappings make output clusters sensitive to weak graph bridges or local relations based on distance in the graph over learned nodes, leaving no explicit constraint on which node groups remain intact during mapping. This paper addresses this gap by proposing PHIDA, a persistence-guided node-to-cluster mapping method for online clustering with learned nodes. PHIDA implements this mapping within Adaptive Resonance Theory (ART)-based online clustering by combining Inverse-Distance ART (IDA) node learning with node-to-cluster mapping constrained by Persistent Homology (PH). Experiments on 24 benchmark datasets show that PHIDA achieves the best average ranks in stationary comparisons that include the recent stationary-only clustering methods, while also improving aggregate performance in the nonstationary setting over the evaluated online methods that adaptively create and update nodes. Ablations and comparisons with conventional node-to-cluster mappings indicate that the observed gains are associated with PH-constrained mapping that preserves raw PH components, together with the use of the PH component view during node learning. Source code is available at https://github.com/Masuyama-lab/PHIDA

</details>


### [102] [Event Fields: Learning Latent Event Structure for Waveform Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08685)
*Li Na,Yuanyun Zhang,Shi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类新的波形基础模型，将生理时间序列建模为潜在事件过程的实现，而非传统的序列化表示。通过自监督学习框架，在随机分割和时间-频率投影上强制一致性，学习事件级表征。在心律失常分类、血流动力学预测和波形检索等任务上，相比强序列基线方法，在性能、鲁棒性和标签效率上均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统波形基础模型将生理信号视为局部token或patch的序列，忽略了临床上有意义的结构源于时间上延展的、相互作用的事件，且事件的边界和动态不可直接观测。现有方法缺乏对这种事件级组织结构的归纳偏置。

Method: 提出自监督学习框架，通过随机分割和时频投影的一致性约束学习表征；设计分段感知编码器和潜在交互算子，捕捉推断事件间的依赖关系；可自然扩展到多模态对齐场景。

Result: 在心律失常分类、血流动力学预测、波形检索等多个生理基准任务上，相比强序列基线方法，性能、鲁棒性和标签效率均有提升。

Conclusion: 从信号中心表示转向事件中心表示为建模生理动态提供了更合适的归纳偏置，为扩展医疗领域基础模型提供了互补路径。

Abstract: We propose a new class of waveform foundation models that departs from conventional sequence based representations by modeling physiological time series as realizations of latent event processes. Rather than treating signals as collections of local tokens or patches, our approach assumes that clinically meaningful structure arises from temporally extended, interacting events whose boundaries and dynamics are not directly observed. To capture this structure, we introduce a self supervised learning framework that enforces consistency across stochastic segmentations and time frequency projections of the same waveform, encouraging representations that are invariant to signal level perturbations while preserving event level organization. The resulting model combines a segmentation aware encoder with a latent interaction operator that captures dependencies among inferred events, and naturally extends to multimodal settings by aligning modalities through shared event representations. Across a range of physiological benchmarks, including arrhythmia classification, hemodynamic prediction, and waveform retrieval, the proposed method improves performance, robustness, and label efficiency relative to strong sequence based baselines. These results suggest that shifting from signal centric to event centric representations provides a more appropriate inductive bias for modeling physiological dynamics and offers a complementary path to scaling foundation models in healthcare.

</details>


### [103] [Structure-Centric Graph Foundation Model via Geometric Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08689)
*Xiaodong He,Haolan He,Ruiyi Fang,Ming Sun,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于结构的图基础模型SCGFM，通过可学习的几何基和Gromov-Wasserstein距离对齐异构拓扑，并引入结构感知特征重编码机制解决特征不兼容问题，在图级和节点级任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图基础模型受限于图结构异质性和节点特征空间不兼容，难以学习可跨域迁移的通用表示。

Method: 将图建模为度量测度空间，引入可学习几何基作为共享坐标系统；通过Gromov-Wasserstein距离将图对齐到几何基，得到结构对齐的潜在表示；提出结构感知特征重编码机制统一节点表示，无需固定特征维度或数据集特定预处理。

Result: 在图级和节点级任务上展现出强大的域内和跨域泛化能力，优于现有GFM方法。

Conclusion: 以图拓扑结构为核心传递可迁移知识，结合几何对齐与特征重编码，可有效构建跨异构图的通用基础模型。

Abstract: Graph foundation models (GFMs) seek transferable representations across graph domains but are limited by structural heterogeneity and incompatible node feature spaces. We propose Structure-Centric Graph Foundation Models (SCGFM), which treat graph topology as the primary source of transferable knowledge. Modeling graphs as metric measure spaces, SCGFM introduces learnable geometric bases that define a shared structural coordinate system. Graphs are aligned to these bases via Gromov-Wasserstein distances, yielding structure-aligned latent representations that accommodate heterogeneous graph topologies. To address feature incompatibility, SCGFM employs a structure-aware feature re-encoding mechanism that unifies node representations without assuming a fixed feature dimensionality or requiring dataset-specific preprocessing. Experiments on graph- and node-level tasks demonstrate strong in-domain and cross-domain generalization, outperforming existing GFM approaches.

</details>


### [104] [AAAC: Activation-Aware Adaptive Codebooks for 4-bit LLM Weight Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08692)
*Beshr IslamBouli,David Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AAAC方法，利用每层两个小型学习型码本（共64字节）替代固定标量码本进行4比特权重量化，通过激活感知重建误差选择最优码本，零存储开销，量化时间仅需3-30分钟，在多个模型上优于AWQ、GPTQ等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4比特权重量化方法（如AWQ、GPTQ）在固定网格上的映射能力有限，而更高精度的方法（如OmniQuant、QuIP#）需数小时量化时间。本文旨在设计一种轻量级、高精度且量化时间短的4比特LLM权重量化方法。

Method: AAAC为每层引入两个小型可学习标量码本（共64字节），每个权重组根据激活加权重建误差选择最优码本，并将码本选择编码在该组正缩放因子的未用符号位中，实现零额外存储开销。

Result: AAAC在单GPU上3-30分钟内完成量化，在多个模型家族上与AWQ、GPTQ、IF4、GPTVQ、OmniQuant、SqueezeLLM、QuIP#等基线方法相比，以数量级更少的量化时间取得了更优的性能。

Conclusion: AAAC是一种轻量级、高效的4比特LLM权重量化方法，通过激活感知自适应码本在极短量化时间内实现优异精度，且无额外存储开销。

Abstract: Post-training weight-only quantization to 4 bits is widely used to reduce the memory and compute costs of large language model inference. Existing PTQ methods, such as AWQ and GPTQ, improve how weights are mapped onto a fixed 4-bit grid through scaling, clipping, or error compensation. To further improve accuracy, methods such as OmniQuant and QuIP\# uses gradient-assisted algorithms at the cost of hours of quantization time. In this work, we propose AAAC (Activation-Aware Adaptive Codebooks), a lightweight method for 4-bit LLM weight quantization. AAAC replaces the fixed scalar codebook used in standard quantization with two small learned scalar codebooks (64 bytes) per layer. Each group of weights selects the codebook that minimizes activation-weighted reconstruction error, encoding the choice in the unused sign bit of the group's positive scale and adding zero storage overhead. AAAC completes in 3--30 minutes on a single GPU, and adds no memory beyond the model itself. We evaluate against AWQ, GPTQ, IF4, GPTVQ, OmniQuant, SqueezeLLM, and QuIP\# across model families. AAAC outperforms baselines at orders-of-magnitude less quantization time.

</details>


### [105] [METBRA25Y: Brazil Surface Meteorology Archive with Harmonized Variables and Quality Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08701)
*Matheus Lima Castro,William Dantas Vichete,Leopoldo Lusquino Filho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了METBRA25Y数据集，这是一个基于巴西国家气象研究所（INMET）历史公共记录构建的标准化逐时地面气象观测档案，涵盖2000-2025年间616个台站的观测数据，经过严格的质量控制流程处理，适用于环境、气候、水文、农业、城市风险及机器学习等研究领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巴西现有气象观测数据存在列名不统一、元数据不完整、质量控制标准不一致等问题，难以支撑可重复性的科学研究。研究者需要一个经过标准化处理、带有明确质量控制元数据的台站级气象时间序列数据集。

Method: 数据集的构建流程包括：读取INMET年度归档文件、从原始文件头解析台站元数据、将异构的葡萄牙语列名规范化为统一模式、构建逐时时间戳、按城市和台站整合观测数据，最终输出压缩CSV文件及台站清单、逐台站质量标记、日降水量聚合、变量级失败汇总和缺失数据审计。质量控制采用两阶段策略：首先将物理上不可能的值转换为缺失值并标记；其次通过时间一致性和跨变量一致性检查生成诊断标记，但不覆盖原始测量值。

Result: 数据集覆盖2000-2025年，包含616个唯一台站编码（其中605个台站坐标位于巴西合理范围内），涵盖降水、气温、露点温度、相对湿度、气压、风速、阵风、风向和太阳总辐射等关键气象变量。数据集以标准化格式发布，包含完整的质量控制标记和元数据。

Conclusion: METBRA25Y数据集为巴西提供了首个大规模、标准化、带有明确质量控制元数据的逐时气象观测档案，有助于推动巴西气象相关领域的可重复性研究。论文详细记录了数据集的来源、文件组织、标准化模式、质量控制规则、技术验证结果、局限性及推荐使用规范。

Abstract: This data paper describes METBRA25Y, a harmonized archive of hourly surface meteorological observations from Brazil derived from public historical records of the Instituto Nacional de Meteorologia (INMET). The dataset was designed to support reproducible environmental, climatological, hydrological, agricultural, urban-risk, and machine-learning studies that require station-level meteorological time series with standardized variable names and explicit quality-control metadata. The processing workflow ingests annual INMET archives, parses station metadata from raw file headers, normalizes heterogeneous Portuguese column names into a canonical schema, constructs hourly timestamps, consolidates observations by city and station, and exports compressed CSV files together with station manifests, per-station quality flags, daily precipitation aggregates, variable-level failure summaries, and missing-data audits. The quality-control protocol follows a two-stage strategy: first, physically implausible values are converted to missing values and flagged; second, temporal and cross-variable consistency checks generate diagnostic flags without necessarily overwriting the original measurements. The resulting package covers observations between 2000 and 2025, with stationspecific temporal coverage, and includes key meteorological variables such as precipitation, air temperature, dew point, relative humidity, atmospheric pressure, wind speed, wind gust, wind direction, and global solar radiation. Based on the summary files included in the current release snapshot, the archive contains 616 unique station codes across variable summaries, of which 605 have coordinates within a broad Brazil plausibility envelope. This paper documents the dataset provenance, file organization, harmonized schema, quality-control rules, technical validation outputs, limitations, and recommended usage practices.

</details>


### [106] [Classification-Head Bias in Class-Level Machine Unlearning: Diagnosis, Mitigation, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08730)
*Weidong Zheng,Kongyang Chen,Yuanwei Guo,Yatie Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示类别级机器遗忘中存在一个“偏置主导的捷径”：通过降低分类头中的偏置项即可抑制被遗忘类别的预测，传统评估指标无法察觉这种内部机制。作者提出BiasShift诊断基线，并设计了两种偏置感知机制（TS-BGRM和LB-HR）及三种偏置导向评估指标（BSC、MBG、MBS），在多个数据集上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类别级机器遗忘评估指标（保留集准确率、遗忘集准确率、遗忘时间）仅关注外部表现，无法揭示模型内部如何实现遗忘。作者发现了偏置主导的捷径问题，即通过降低分类头偏置项即可让模型“看似”遗忘了指定类别，但实际留下了可被利用的偏置异常模式。需要更深入理解遗忘的内部机制并设计相应的缓解与评估方法。

Method: 1) 分析softmax交叉熵训练下分类头偏置的梯度动力学，解释为什么仅优化保留集会导致缺失类别的偏置降低。2) 提出BiasShift诊断基线，通过简单的偏置操作即可满足传统遗忘指标。3) 设计两种偏置感知机制：两阶段偏置梯度反转机制（TS-BGRM）和下界铰链正则化（LB-HR）。4) 提出三种偏置导向指标：偏置稳定系数（BSC）、中位偏置差距（MBG）、最小偏置分数（MBS）。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和Tiny-ImageNet上的实验表明，所提方法在保持竞争力的遗忘性能的同时，能够产生更稳定的偏置分布。

Conclusion: 类别级遗忘存在偏置捷径这一隐蔽问题，需要从内部偏置分布的角度重新审视遗忘评估；提出的偏置感知机制和偏置导向评估指标为更可靠的机器遗忘提供了新方向。

Abstract: Class-level machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of specified classes while preserving model utility on retained classes. Existing methods are commonly evaluated by retain-set accuracy, forget-set accuracy, and unlearning time, but these metrics provide limited insight into how forgetting is achieved internally. In this paper, we reveal a bias-dominated shortcut in class-level unlearning: the prediction of forgotten classes can be suppressed by decreasing the corresponding bias terms in the final classification head. We first analyze the gradient dynamics of classification-head biases under softmax cross-entropy training, explaining why retain-set-only optimization tends to reduce the biases of absent classes. Based on this observation, we introduce BiasShift as a diagnostic baseline, showing that simple bias manipulation can satisfy conventional unlearning metrics while leaving abnormal bias patterns that reveal forgotten labels. To mitigate excessive forgotten-class bias suppression, we propose two bias-aware mechanisms, namely Two-Stage Bias Gradient Reversal Mechanism (TS-BGRM) and Lower-Bound Hinge Regularization (LB-HR). We further introduce three bias-oriented metrics, including Bias Stability Coefficient (BSC), Median Bias Gap (MBG), and Minimal Bias Score (MBS), to quantify bias dependence and potential leakage. Experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Tiny-ImageNet demonstrate that the proposed methods maintain competitive unlearning performance while producing more stable bias distributions. We have released our code at {https://github.com/zwd2024/Beyond-the-Shadow-of-Bias-From-Classification-Head-Bias-to-Parameter-Redistribution}.

</details>


### [107] [SlimQwen: Exploring the Pruning and Distillation in Large MoE Model Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08738)
*Shengkun Tang,Zekun Wang,Bo Zheng,Liangyu Wang,Rui Men,Siqi Zhang,Xiulong Yuan,Zihan Qiu,Zhiqiang Shen,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了大规模预训练场景下混合专家（MoE）模型的压缩方法，涵盖结构化剪枝与知识蒸馏。主要发现包括：剪枝预训练MoE优于从头训练；不同一次性专家压缩方法在大规模持续预训练后性能趋同；提出了部分保留专家合并策略；将知识蒸馏与语言建模损失结合优于单独使用蒸馏；提出了多token预测蒸馏；渐进式剪枝调度优于一次性压缩。最终将Qwen3-Next-80A3B压缩至23A2B模型并保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化剪枝和知识蒸馏是压缩大语言模型的典型技术，但它们在预训练规模下、尤其是针对混合专家（MoE）模型的应用尚不明确，缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 系统研究了三个关键问题：剪枝是否比从头训练提供更好的初始化、专家压缩选择如何影响持续训练后的最终模型、哪种训练策略最有效。提出了部分保留专家合并策略和多token预测蒸馏方法。

Result: 剪枝预训练MoE优于从头训练；不同一次性专家压缩方法在大规模持续预训练后性能趋同；结合KD与语言建模损失优于单独使用KD；渐进式剪枝调度优于一次性压缩；成功将Qwen3-Next-80A3B压缩至23A2B。

Conclusion: 为大规模MoE模型压缩提供了实用指导，证明了渐进式剪枝、特定蒸馏策略和专家合并方法的有效性。

Abstract: Structured pruning and knowledge distillation (KD) are typical techniques for compressing large language models, but it remains unclear how they should be applied at pretraining scale, especially to recent mixture-of-experts (MoE) models. In this work, we systematically study MoE compression in large-scale pretraining, focusing on three key questions: whether pruning provides a better initialization than training from scratch, how expert compression choices affect the final model after continued training, and which training strategy is most effective. We have the following findings: First, across depth, width, and expert compression, pruning a pretrained MoE consistently outperforms training the target architecture from scratch under the same training budget. Second, different one-shot expert compression methods converge to similar final performance after large-scale continual pretraining. Motivated by this, we introduce a simple partial-preservation expert merging strategy that improves downstream performance across most benchmarks. Third, combining KD with the language modeling loss outperforms KD alone, particularly on knowledge-intensive tasks. We further propose multi-token prediction (MTP) distillation, which yields consistent gains. Finally, given the same training tokens, progressive pruning schedules outperform one-shot compression, suggesting that gradual architecture transitions lead to better optimization trajectories. Putting it all together, we compress Qwen3-Next-80A3B to a 23A2B model that retains competitive performance. These results offer practical guidance for efficient MoE compression at scale.

</details>


### [108] [The Wristband Gaussian Loss: Deterministic, Composable Latents via a Sphere-Interval Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08749)
*Mikhail Parakhin,André M. Carvalho,Patrick Haluptzok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Wristband Gaussian Loss，一种确定性批量损失函数，通过将点嵌入映射到腕带空间（球面×[0,1]）实现高斯化，无需采样、KL项或迭代传输，并在Lean4中形式化证明。基于该损失构建了确定性高斯自编码器，用于反事实采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯化方法（如正态化流、对抗训练、最优传输等）通常需要采样、KL散度计算或迭代运输，计算复杂且缺乏确定性保证。本文旨在设计一种确定性的、可证明正确的批量损失函数，将任意点嵌入映射为标准高斯分布，同时保持计算高效性。

Method: 1) 提出Wristband映射：将x映射为方向u=x/∥x∥和CDF变换半径t=F_χ²_d(∥x∥²)，形成腕带空间S^{d-1}×[0,1]；2) 证明推前测度等于均匀乘积分布当且仅当源分布为N(0,I_d)；3) 设计Neumann反射腕带斥力能量作为目标函数，两种计算方式：最近三图像成对截断(O(N²d))和谱Neumann路径(O(NdK))；4) 加1D Wasserstein径向项和矩惩罚作为有限样本加速器；5) 蒙特卡洛零校准将各分量合并为单一标准化统计量；6) 构建确定性高斯自编码器（DGA），结合可学习键欧几里得注意力与精确可逆流。

Result: 在axis-uniform X基准上，Wristband在2D上具有竞争力，在10D上取得最佳分数。在更难的径向-角-连接函数（边缘分布正确但存在依赖关系）上，Wristband在10D和128D上均取得最佳分数。在Lean4中机器验证了核心定理。

Conclusion: Wristband Gaussian Loss提供了一种确定性的、可证明正确的高斯化方法，避免了传统方法的采样和迭代开销。结合DGA框架，可实现对潜在空间的精确控制，支持反事实采样中的独立因子和依赖因子的上下文/残差构造。

Abstract: We present the Wristband Gaussian Loss, a deterministic batch loss for Gaussianizing point embeddings without sampling, KL terms, or iterative transport. Each $x \in \mathbb{R}^d$ is mapped to a direction $u = x/\|x\|$ and a CDF-transformed radius $t = F_{χ^2_d}(\|x\|^2)$ on the wristband $S^{d-1} \times [0,1]$. We prove (and machine-verify in Lean~4) that for $d \ge 2$ the pushforward wristband map equals $σ_{d-1} \otimes \mathrm{Unif}[0,1]$ iff the source is $\mathcal{N}(0, I_d)$, and that the Neumann-reflected wristband repulsion energy is uniquely minimized at the uniform target. We compute this reflected-kernel objective in two ways: a nearest three-image pairwise truncation at $O(N^2 d)$, and a spectral Neumann path joining angular and radial Mercer modes (spherical-harmonic and cosine) at $O(N d K)$, with empirically matched gradients. A 1D Wasserstein radial term and a moment penalty serve as finite-sample accelerators with the same optimum, and Monte-Carlo null calibration turns the components into a single standardized statistic. We evaluate direct point-cloud Gaussianization with a calibrated barycentric $W_2$ score: a deterministic Gaussian reference batch is built by recursive Hungarian averaging, with each method reported as a $z$-score against same-size Gaussian batches. On the axis-uniform X benchmark, Wristband is competitive in 2D and gives the best 10D score. On a harder radial--angular-copula impostor whose Gaussian radial and angular marginals are correct but dependent, Wristband gives the best 10D and 128D scores. Coupled with learnable-key Euclidean attention and exact invertible flows, the resulting Deterministic Gaussian Autoencoder delivers a Gaussian-latent interface for counterfactual sampling with independent factors and a context/residual construction for dependent factors.

</details>


### [109] [Communicating Sound Through Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08750)
